under the alternative weight-row-projected non-Nesterov EMA loop [`exact_exp49_opt=False`, `exp59` Arms 1–2], 360-step `rake_attn` scores `2.0548 ± 0.0007` at $\eta_K=0.012$ and `2.0545 ± 0.0008` at $\eta_K=0.024$).
   - **720-Step Fixed-Token Scaling ($M=2$, Pure $M=4$ vs. $M=4+\text{ve\_gate}$, Unconfounded $\eta_K=0.024$ Controls, `Wiener-MMSE` / `Hadamard-4` / `Sparse Top-K=32` Key Gather, Group Granularity $g \in \{1, 2, 3, 6\}$, & Exact FP32 SVD Deconfounding), Main-RNG-Matched & Layer-RNG-Synchronized $2 \times 2$ Block-Depth Factorials, Credit-Engine Depth-Asymmetry Deconfounding, Fixed-Probe Upper-Half $M=4$ Reallocation, Compositional/Bus/Clock/Leverage-Inversion Controls, and Same-Trainer Matched-Training-Region-Time Controls (`exp49`–`exp51`, `exp53`–`exp54`, `exp56`–`exp60`, `exp62`–`exp64`, `exp66`, `exp67`):**
     1. **720-Step Fixed-Token Scaling, Pure $M=4$ vs. `ve_gate` Isolation, Unconfounded $\eta_K=0.024$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ Controls (`1.9671 ± 0.0006` i.i.d.; `1.9675 ± 0.0010` `Hadamard-4`), Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` / `Hadamard-4` / `Sparse Top-K=32` Key Gather (`1.9632 ± 0.0006` dense in `29.14s`; `1.9633 ± 0.0009` sparse `Top-K=32` in `24.36s`; `1.9632 ± 0.0009` `Hadamard-4 + LevInv` in `24.54s`), Group Granularity $g \in \{1, 2, 3, 6\}$ (`1.9634 -> 1.9615 [g=3] -> 1.9599 [g=2] -> 1.9565 [g=1]` in `23.79s` [`Hadamard-4`] & `1.9566 ± 0.0002` in `23.68s` [`Top-K=32`], `81.7%` of 4-to-6-Mat BP Gain), & 720-Step Exact FP32 SVD $2 \times 2$ Deconfounding (`1.9629 ± 0.0007` `Joint Exact SVD + Global Sphere` vs. `1.9561 ± 0.0008` `Per-Head Exact SVD + Global Sphere` in `107.33s`, `-0.0068 ± 0.0011` BPB contrast, `82.5%` of BP Gain, `exp49`, `exp51`, `exp53`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp63`, `exp64`, `exp66`, `exp67`):** At `720` fixed steps (`1.475M` tokens), evaluating 4-matrix vs. 6-matrix Exact Backpropagation under the co-designed optimizer (`backprop_d6_4mat_720steps_codesigned` vs. `backprop_d6_6mat_720steps_codesigned`, `exp51`) shows that the exact-BP 4-to-6-matrix $(W_Q, W_K)$-unfreezing gain widens **$2.40\times$ from `-0.0216` BPB (`2.0532 -> 2.0316`) at 360 steps to `-0.0519` BPB (`1.9423 ± 0.0031 -> 1.8904 ± 0.0012` DEV BPB; `1.9072 ± 0.0036 -> 1.8551 ± 0.0016` VAL-B) at 720 steps**. Under backward-free Delayed WM, 720-step 4-matrix WM (`wm_d6_4mat_same_trainer_720steps_triton`, `exp51`) scores **`1.9990 / 1.9982 / 1.9996` $\to$ `1.9989 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9646 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `241.96` PPL)** in `15.62s` (matching `exp39`'s `1.9989 ± 0.0008` in `13.57s`), 720-step Full 6-Matrix $M=2$ reaches **`1.9772 / 1.9762 / 1.9755` $\to$ `1.9763 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`1.9416 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `227.41` PPL)** in `21.67s` (`exp49`) and **`1.9750 / 1.9742 / 1.9775` $\to$ `1.9756 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB (`1.9408 ± 0.0019` VAL-B, `226.95` PPL)** in `21.77s` inside the main-RNG-matched `exp50` trainer (`wm_d6_full_6block_qk_m2_isolated_rng_720steps_triton`, `exp53`, widening the fixed-token gain over 4-matrix WM to **`-0.0226` to `-0.0233` BPB = `43.5%`–`44.9%` of the 720-step BP gain**). At $M=4$ under $\gamma_K=0.85, \eta_K=0.012$, while 720-step Full 6-Matrix $M=4 + \text{ve\_gate}$ (`wm_d6_6mat_decoupled_qk_future_g085_m4_plus_ve_gate_720steps_triton`, `exp51`) reaches **`1.9675 / 1.9686 / 1.9687` $\to$ `1.9683 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9334 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `222.45` PPL)** in `28.57s` (`59.0%` of the BP gain), isolating **pure 6-matrix $M=4$ without `ve_gate`** (`wm_d6_6mat_decoupled_qk_future_g085_m4_no_gate_720steps_triton`, `exp53` Arm 2) reaches **`1.9671 / 1.9668 / 1.9677` $\to$ `1.9672 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`1.9324 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `221.80` PPL)** in **`27.71s`** (**`-0.0317` BPB over 4-matrix WM = `61.1%` of the 720-step BP gain**, outperforming $M=4+\text{ve\_gate}$ by `-0.0011 ± 0.0007` BPB across all 3 seeds). Crucially, running the **unconfounded same-rate ($\eta_K=0.024$) $\gamma_K=0.85$ controls at 720 steps** (`exp63` Arms 2 & 3) yields **`1.9670 / 1.9666 / 1.9678` $\to$ `1.9671 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9321 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `221.74` PPL, `23.29s`)** for i.i.d. probes (`wm_d6_6mat_g085_key_m4_lrk024_exact_exp49_720steps_triton`) and **`1.9685 / 1.9665 / 1.9675` $\to$ `1.9675 ± 0.0010` DEV BPB (`1.9325 ± 0.0009` VAL-B, `221.97` PPL, `23.57s`)** for `Hadamard-4` probes (`wm_d6_6mat_g085_hadamard4_qk_m4_lrk024_720steps_triton`)—showing that raising $\eta_K: 0.012 \to 0.024$ under fixed $\gamma_K=0.85$ changes 720-step DEV BPB by only **`-0.0001` BPB** (`1.9672 -> 1.9671`), so **`97.5%` (`-0.0039` of `-0.0040` BPB) of the 720-step `Wiener-MMSE` gain comes from replacing $\gamma_K=0.85$ with `Wiener-MMSE` at matched $\eta_K=0.024$!** Specifically, under `exact_exp49_opt=True, \eta_K=0.024`, 720-step Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ `rake_attn` (`wm_d6_6mat_rake_attn_key_m4_lrk024_exact_exp49_720steps_triton`, `exp59` Arm 11) reaches **`1.9668 / 1.9648 / 1.9659` $\to$ `1.9658 ± 0.0010` DEV BPB (`1.9310 ± 0.0010` VAL-B, `220.96` PPL, `28.97s`)**, dense `wiener_mmse` (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_key_m4_lrk024_exact_exp49_720steps_triton`, `exp59` Arm 12) reaches **`1.9635 / 1.9626 / 1.9636` $\to$ `1.9632 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9284 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `219.39` PPL, `29.14s`; 4-decimal paired seedwise differences `-0.0036, -0.0042, -0.0041` = `-0.00397 ± 0.00032` vs. `exp53` Arm 2 `1.9672`, and `-0.0035, -0.0040, -0.0042` = `-0.00390 ± 0.00036` vs. matched-$\eta_K=0.024$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ `1.9671` [`1.9670 / 1.9666 / 1.9678`])**, `wiener_mmse` + Hadamard-$4$ (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_qk_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp60` Arm 4) reaches **`1.9642 / 1.9627 / 1.9632` $\to$ `1.9634 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB (`1.9284 ± 0.0007` VAL-B, `219.46` PPL, `24.52s`; 4-decimal seedwise `-0.0029, -0.0041, -0.0045` = `-0.00383 ± 0.00083` vs. `1.9672`, and `-0.0043, -0.0038, -0.0043` = `-0.00413 ± 0.00029` vs. matched `Hadamard-4` $\gamma_K=0.85$ `1.9675` [`1.9685 / 1.9665 / 1.9675`])**, `wiener_mmse` + Hadamard-$4$ + Stage-2 Leverage Inversion (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_lev_inv_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp60` Arm 6) reaches **`1.9641 / 1.9623 / 1.9632` $\to$ `1.9632 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`1.9283 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `219.37` PPL, `24.54s`; seedwise `-0.0030, -0.0045, -0.0045` = `-0.0040 ± 0.0009` vs. `1.9672`)**, and **Sparse Top-$K=32$ `wiener_mmse_topk32`** (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_topk32_key_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp60` Arm 5) reaches **`1.9640 / 1.9622 / 1.9636` $\to$ `1.9633 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`1.9285 ± 0.0009` VAL-B, `219.42` PPL, `24.36s`; seedwise `-0.0031, -0.0046, -0.0041` = `-0.0039 ± 0.0008` vs. `1.9672`, `16.4%` faster than dense `29.14s`)**—lifting 720-step 4-to-6-matrix Exact-BP recovery from **`61.1%` (`-0.0317 / -0.0519`) to `68.8%` (`-0.0357 / -0.0519` BPB)**. Finally, evaluating Zhang et al.'s [12] group granularity $g \in \{1, 2, 3, 6\}$ at 720 steps (`exp67` Arms 3–4 & `exp64` Arms 4–5) shows a strict monotonic improvement as $g$ decreases: $g=6$ (`1.9634 ± 0.0008`) $\to$ $g=3$ (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_group3_muon_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp67` Arm 4: **`1.9630 / 1.9600 / 1.9615` $\to$ `1.9615 ± 0.0015` DEV BPB, `1.9264 ± 0.0015` VAL-B, `218.36` PPL, `29.12s`**) $\to$ $g=2$ (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_group2_muon_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp67` Arm 3: **`1.9604 / 1.9597 / 1.9595` $\to$ `1.9599 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB, `1.9247 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `217.37` PPL, `29.06s`**) $\to$ $g=1$ (**`1.9567 / 1.9561 / 1.9568` $\to$ `1.9565 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB, `1.9214 ± 0.0004` VAL-B, `215.39` PPL, `23.79s`** with `Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4`, `exp64` Arm 4; and **`1.9568 / 1.9564 / 1.9566` $\to$ `1.9566 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB, `1.9216 ± 0.0001` VAL-B, `215.43` PPL, `23.68s`** with Sparse `Top-K=32`, `exp64` Arm 5). And in our complete 720-step $2 \times 2$ `{Joint (g=6), Per-Head (g=1)} x {Pre-Polar Muon, Exact FP32 SVD Polar}` deconfounding controls (`exp66` Arms 5–6 & `exp67` Arm 8), **Per-Head Pre-Polar Muon + Global Sphere Restoration** (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_per_head_muon_global_sphere_m4_720steps_triton`) reaches **`1.9569 / 1.9561 / 1.9569` $\to$ `1.9566 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`1.9216 ± 0.0004` VAL-B, `215.46` PPL, `28.96s`, `0.0001` BPB from Per-Head Sphere `1.9565`)**, while under **Pure Exact FP32 SVD Polar Factor [no 2-pass equilibration, no `PolarExpress`, global sphere]** moving from **Joint Exact FP32 SVD ($g=6$, `wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_joint_exact_svd_global_sphere_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp67` Arm 8: `1.9633 / 1.9621 / 1.9632` $\to$ `1.9629 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB, `1.9277 ± 0.0007` VAL-B, `219.18` PPL, `214.95s`)** to **Per-Head Exact FP32 SVD ($g=1$, `wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_per_head_exact_svd_global_sphere_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp66` Arm 6: `1.9570 / 1.9559 / 1.9555` $\to$ `1.9561 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB, `1.9210 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `215.15` PPL, `107.33s`)** improves 720-step validation loss by **`-0.0068 ± 0.0011` DEV BPB (`-0.0067 ± 0.0009` VAL-B, `-4.03` PPL)** across all 3 seeds, closely matching the **`-0.0068` to `-0.0073` DEV BPB** contrast under `Pre-Polar Muon` (`1.9634 -> 1.9566` / `1.9565`) while extending per-head polar orthogonalization to Tier-1 $W_V$ at 720 steps (`Fixed-QK+V`, $g_Q=g_K=g_V=1$, `exp68` Arms 1 & 3) reaches **`1.9550 / 1.9551 / 1.9543` $\to$ `1.9548 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB (`1.9198 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `214.38` PPL, `23.86s`, `-0.0017 ± 0.0006` BPB over `Fixed-QK` `1.9565`)** under `Pre-Polar Muon` (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_per_head_qkv_muon_m4_720steps_triton`) and **`1.9549 / 1.9542 / 1.9538` $\to$ `1.9543 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9192 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `214.08` PPL, `127.30s`, `-0.0018 ± 0.0002` BPB over `Per-Head QK Exact SVD` `1.9561` and `-0.0086 ± 0.0009` BPB over `Joint Exact SVD` `1.9629`)** under **Pure Exact FP32 SVD Polar** (`wm_d6_6mat_wiener_mmse_hadamard4_per_head_qkv_exact_svd_global_sphere_m4_720steps_triton`)—lifting 720-step 4-to-6-matrix attention-routing recovery to **`81.7%` (`Per-Head QK Pre-Polar`), `82.5%` (`Per-Head QK Exact SVD`), `85.0%` (`-0.0441 / -0.0519` BPB, `Per-Head QKV Pre-Polar`), and `85.9%` (`-0.0446 / -0.0519` BPB, `Per-Head QKV Exact SVD`)** of the shared-LR Global-BP gain (`1.9423 -> 1.8904`), **`48.5%`, `49.0%`, `49.7%` (`-0.0441 / -0.0887`), and `50.3%` (`-0.0446 / -0.0887`)** of the `Per-Head Global-BP` gain (`1.9423 -> 1.8549/1.8536`), and **`48.1%`, `48.6%`, `50.1%` (`-0.0441 / -0.0881`), and `50.6%` (`-0.0446 / -0.0881`)** of the `Per-Head Sub-Block Surrogate-BP` gain (`1.9819 -> 1.8938`, `exp68` Arm 9) (while under the alternative weight-row-projected non-Nesterov EMA loop [`exact_exp49_opt=False`, `exp59` Arms 3–4], 720-step `rake_attn` scores `1.9733 ± 0.0014` on Upper-Half $M=4$ and `1.9697 ± 0.0011` on Full 6-Matrix $M=4$).
     2. **Why Full-Depth SDPA Probing Loses to 4-Matrix at Matched Training-Region Time (`exp49`–`exp51`):** Inside the `exp49`–`exp51` trainer, evaluating $2M$ perturbed SDPA calls across all 6 blocks increases per-step training-region time from `21.46–21.65 ms/step` (4-matrix) to `22.83 ms/step` ($M=1$) and `25.97–30.10 ms/step` ($M=2$). At the short 360-step budget, 4-matrix controls given the same measured training-region GPU time (`384` steps in `8.20s` vs. `8.22s` for $M=1$; `438` steps in `9.39s` vs. `9.35s` for $M=2$) reach **`2.0598 ± 0.0005`** and **`2.0454 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB**, outperforming 360-step 6-matrix $M=1$ (`2.0631`) and $M=2$ (`2.0595`). At the longer 720-step horizon, while 720-step Full 6-Matrix $M=2$ (`1.9763 ± 0.0009` in `21.67s` / `1.9756 ± 0.0017` in `21.77s`) beats an `875`-step 4-matrix control (`wm_d6_4mat_equal_compute_720m2_875steps_triton`, `exp50`, calibrated to the 360-step $+21.5\%$ step-time ratio: `1.9819 / 1.9815 / 1.9828` $\to$ **`1.9821 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB, `1.9485 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `231.03` PPL** in `18.91s`) by `-0.0058` to `-0.0065` DEV BPB, granting 4-matrix the **full `21.55s` (`1,004` steps)** inside the same trainer (`wm_d6_4mat_equal_time_720m2_1004steps_triton`, `exp51`) reaches **`1.9718 / 1.9715 / 1.9726` $\to$ `1.9720 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9392 ± 0.0004` VAL-B, `224.73` PPL)** in `21.55s`—still edging out 720-step Full 6-Matrix $M=2$ (`1.9763` / `1.9756`) by `-0.0036` to `-0.0043` BPB.
      3. **Main-RNG-Matched & Per-Layer-RNG-Synchronized 720-Step $2 \times 2$ `{Lower-Half (0,1,2), Upper-Half (3,4,5)}` Depth Factorials, Fixed-Probe Upper-Half $M=4$ Reallocation (`1.9699 ± 0.0001` [`g085`] & `1.9672 ± 0.0017` [`wiener_mmse`]), and Same-Trainer Matched-Time Advantage (`1.9670 ± 0.0002` at 740st $M=4$; `1.9609 ± 0.0006` [`g085`], `1.9594 ± 0.0019` [`wiener_mmse + topk32`], & `1.9524 ± 0.0004` [`Per-Head Stiefel + wiener_mmse + topk32`] at 840st $M=2$, `exp50`–`exp51`, `exp53`–`exp54`, `exp56`, `exp59`, `exp62`, `exp64`):** Why does uniform 6-block probing underperform at matched training-region time? Evaluating our 720-step $2 \times 2$ depth-ablation factorial both in `train_d6_exp50_arm` (`exp51` + `exp53` Arm 1, $M=2$, where query/key draws use isolated CUDA RNGs so the **main model/data RNG stream is bit-for-bit identical** across all 4 arms) and in `train_d6_layer_rng_synced_arm` (`exp56` Arms 11–12, which additionally advances the query/key generators at skipped blocks so **every active block's $(\xi_q, \xi_k)$ perturbation tensors are also bit-for-bit identical** to Full 6-Block $M=2$) reveals a consistent depth gradient in attention-routing utility:
         - **4-Matrix (`none` active Q/K, `24.58M` active):** `1.9990 / 1.9982 / 1.9996` $\to$ `1.9989 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9646 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `241.96` PPL, `15.62s` run GPU)
         - **Lower-Half Blocks `(0, 1, 2)` Only (`25.46M` active):** `1.9826 / 1.9829 / 1.9841` $\to$ **`1.9832 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB (`1.9487 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `231.75` PPL)** in `18.90s` (`exp51` main-RNG-matched, `-0.0157` BPB over 4-matrix); and `1.9849 / 1.9826 / 1.9852` $\to$ **`1.9842 ± 0.0014` DEV BPB (`1.9497 ± 0.0014` VAL-B, `232.42` PPL)** in `16.06s` (`exp56` layer-RNG-synced, `-0.0147` BPB over 4-matrix)
         - **Long-Window `L` Blocks `(3, 5)` Only (`wm_d6_long_window_35_qk_m2_720steps_triton`, `25.17M` active):** `1.9802 / 1.9793 / 1.9788` $\to$ **`1.9794 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9449 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `229.36` PPL)** in `17.82s` (`-0.0195` BPB over 4-matrix with only 2 blocks probed!)
         - **Upper-Half Blocks `(3, 4, 5)` Only (`25.46M` active):** `1.9786 / 1.9775 / 1.9775` $\to$ **`1.9779 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9431 ± 0.0007` VAL-B, `228.40` PPL)** in **`18.59s`** (`exp51` main-RNG-matched, **`-0.0210` BPB over 4-matrix = `90.1%`–`92.9%` of the full 6-block gain at half the SDPA probe overhead**); and `1.9757 / 1.9757 / 1.9793` $\to$ **`1.9769 ± 0.0021` DEV BPB (`1.9421 ± 0.0023` VAL-B, `227.78` PPL)** in `16.09s` (`exp56` layer-RNG-synced, **`-0.0220` BPB = `94.4%` of the full 6-block gain**!)
         - **Full 6-Block `(0..5)` (`wm_d6_full_6block_qk_m2_isolated_rng_720steps_triton`, `exp53` Arm 1, & `wm_d6_6mat_decoupled_qk_future_g085_m2_720steps_triton`, `exp49`, `26.35M` active):** `1.9750 / 1.9742 / 1.9775` $\to$ **`1.9756 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB (`1.9408 ± 0.0019` VAL-B, `226.95` PPL)** in `21.77s` (`exp53` main/layer-RNG-matched) and `1.9772 / 1.9762 / 1.9755` $\to$ `1.9763 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`1.9416 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `227.41` PPL) in `21.67s` (`exp49`).
         *(Note on interpretation: Upper-half versus lower-half probing also alters the attention-window composition across the SSSLSL layout—where blocks `3, 5` are full-context `L` and block `4` is short-window `S`, whereas blocks `0, 1, 2` are all short-window `S`).*
         Thus, **once Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $(W_Q, W_K)$ is active, also probing Lower-Half `(0, 1, 2)` adds only `-0.0013` to `-0.0023` DEV BPB while costing `+3.08s–3.18s` (`+16.6%` training-region time)**. We test two distinct ways to reallocate that saved compute:
         - **Fixed-Token (`720` Steps), Fixed-24-SDPA-Probe/Step Reallocation (`wm_d6_upper_half_qk_m4_720steps_triton`, `exp53` Arm 3; `wm_d6_upper_half_wiener_mmse_key_m4_lrk024_exact_exp49_720steps_triton`, `exp59` Arm 10; & `740`-Step Extension `exp54` Arm 3):** Concentrating the exact same 24 perturbed SDPA calls/step on Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` ($M=4$ on blocks `3, 4, 5`; $M=0$ on blocks `0, 1, 2`) at **fixed `720` steps** achieves **`1.9700 / 1.9698 / 1.9698` $\to$ `1.9699 ± 0.0001` DEV BPB (`1.9349 ± 0.0001` VAL-B, `223.42` PPL)** in **`21.05s`** under $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`exp53` Arm 3)—outperforming uniform 720-step Full 6-Block $M=2$ (`1.9756 ± 0.0017` in `21.77s` / `1.9763 ± 0.0009` in `21.67s`) by **`-0.0057` to `-0.0064` DEV BPB** at equal tokens and lower time, and outperforming 1,004-step 4-matrix (`1.9720 ± 0.0006` in `21.55s`) by **`-0.0021` DEV BPB (`-0.0043` VAL-B BPB)** in `0.50s` less time—and under `wiener_mmse` (`exp59` Arm 10) achieves **`1.9690 / 1.9656 / 1.9670` $\to$ `1.9672 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB (`1.9322 ± 0.0017` VAL-B, `221.79` PPL)** in **`22.36s`**, matching all-6-block $M=4$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`1.9672 ± 0.0005` in `27.71s`) at half the SDPA probes! Running Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$ ($\gamma_K=0.85$) for `740` steps (`wm_d6_upper_half_qk_m4_equal_time_740steps_triton`, `exp54` Arm 3, `19.19s`) reaches **`1.9669 / 1.9669 / 1.9673` $\to$ `1.9670 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB (`1.9321 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `221.69` PPL)**.
         - **Same-Trainer `~20.0–21.7s` Matched-Time Token Extension (`wm_d6_upper_half_qk_m2_equal_time_840steps_triton`, `exp51`; `wm_d6_upper_half_wiener_mmse_topk32_qk_m2_840steps_triton`, `exp62` Arm 4; & `wm_d6_upper_half_wiener_mmse_topk32_per_head_muon_m2_840steps_triton`, `exp64` Arm 6):** Spending the saved time on `120` additional steps of Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=2$ (`840` steps) reaches **`1.9615 / 1.9609 / 1.9604` $\to$ `1.9609 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9264 ± 0.0007` VAL-B BPB, `218.03` PPL)** in **`21.71s`** under $\gamma_K=0.85$ (`exp51`), **`1.9612 / 1.9574 / 1.9597` $\to$ `1.9594 ± 0.0019` DEV BPB (`1.9250 ± 0.0020` VAL-B, `217.13` PPL)** in **`20.21s`** under Joint $(D,D)$ `Wiener-MMSE + Top-K=32` (`exp62` Arm 4), and **`1.9528 / 1.9520 / 1.9525` $\to$ `1.9524 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB (`1.9177 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `212.99` PPL)** in **`19.91s`** under **Per-Head Stiefel `PolarExpress` + `Wiener-MMSE + Top-K=32`** (`exp64` Arm 6)—**outperforming 1,004-step 4-matrix (`1.9720 ± 0.0006` in `21.55s`) by `-0.0196` DEV BPB (`-0.0215` VAL-B BPB, `-11.74` PPL) in `1.64s` less GPU time, and outperforming 720-step Full 6-Matrix Per-Head Stiefel $M=4$ (`1.9565 ± 0.0004` in `23.79s`) by `-0.0041` DEV BPB in `3.88s` less time!**
         - **Stage-2 Terminal-Bus Multi-Probe (`M_bus = 2`), Stage-2 `RMSNorm` Leverage Inversion (`exp60` Arms 3 & 6), 720-Step `both_stages_pre_install` Clock Controls (`exp59` Arms 5–6), Same-Clock Compositional-Relay Diagnostic Controls (`exp53` Arm 4, `exp54` Arms 1, 2, 4), Idealized Block-Skip vs. Sub-Block Direct-Skip Surrogate BP & Exact-`exp49`-Optimizer Controls (`exp57`, `exp58`), and 720-Step Credit-Engine Depth-Asymmetry Deconfounding (`exp63` Arms 4–7):** Adding a second independent Rademacher probe at the terminal `RMSNorm` bus (`wm_d6_upper_half_qk_m4_bus_m2_720steps_triton`, `exp54` Arm 2: `1.9702 / 1.9699 / 1.9699` $\to$ **`1.9700 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB, `1.9350 ± 0.0001` VAL-B** in `19.25s`) matches single-probe `M_bus = 1` (`1.9699 ± 0.0001`) to within `0.0001` BPB, which is compatible with Proposition 1(c)'s result that Stage-2 cross-coordinate `RMSNorm` variance is $O(1/D) \approx 0.0026$ at $D=384$. Likewise, at 720 steps under the co-designed optimizer ($\epsilon = 10^{-4}, \text{lrm}: 1.0 \to 0.2$, `exp59` Arms 5–6), `both_stages_pre_install` (`Stage 2: (t-1, t-1, t), Stage 1: (t-2, t-1, t)`) scores **`1.9979 / 1.9970 / 1.9983` $\to$ `1.9977 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9635 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `241.18` PPL, `22.63s`)** on 4-matrix (`-0.0012` BPB vs. `strict_post_install` `1.9989 ± 0.0007`) and **`1.9687 / 1.9693 / 1.9697` $\to$ `1.9692 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`1.9346 ± 0.0004` VAL-B, `223.02` PPL, `39.77s`)** on 6-matrix $M=4$ (`+0.0020` BPB vs. `strict_post_install` `1.9672 ± 0.0005`), confirming that $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ plus drift annealing keeps post-installation drift bounded within `0.0020` BPB at 720 steps. Similarly, augmenting Stage-2 (`mlp.c_proj, attn.c_proj`) terminal-skip credit with outgoing-column WM rewards from downstream blocks' `mlp.c_fc` matrices (constructed in a descending block loop at clock $t$ from origin-$t-2$ cached traces and supplied to earlier blocks' Stage-2 writer accumulators within the same construction call prior to `mlp.c_fc` installation, and therefore classified separately as `same-clock compositional-relay diagnostic controls` rather than strictly delayed zero-relay WM arms) yields `1.9779 ± 0.0006` (`exp53` Arm 4, shared `xi_bus`, $\alpha_{\text{comp}}=0.20$, `19.37s`), `1.9780 ± 0.0008` (`exp54` Arm 1, independent $\xi_{\text{comp}} \perp \xi_{\text{bus}}$, $\alpha_{\text{comp}}=0.05$, `20.25s`), and `1.9787 ± 0.0008` (`exp54` Arm 4, RMS-norm-matched $\alpha_{\text{comp}}=0.15 \|\hat{g}_{\text{bus}}\|/\|\hat{g}_{\text{comp}}\|$, `19.49s`) vs. `1.9779 ± 0.0006` for pure terminal-skip routing ($\alpha_{\text{comp}}=0$)—showing that two-hop Rademacher variance is roughly $12\times$ smaller in RMS norm than single-hop terminal-skip credit and slightly degrades Stage-2 writer alignment when scaled up. To compare our 3-stage pipeline against idealized direct-to-head backpropagation surrogates while explicitly accounting for (a) the Proposition 1(c) coordinate-energy factor $\mathbb{E}_{\xi}[P_x\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}] = \frac{1}{\text{rms}(z)}P_x\operatorname{diag}(1 - x_i^2/D)a \neq \frac{1}{\text{rms}(z)}P_x a = \mu_{\text{skip}}$ (verified by exact 8-pattern enumeration at $D=3, x=(\sqrt{3/2},\sqrt{3/2},0)^\top, a=(1,0,1)^\top$, where $\mathbb{E}[P_x\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}]=(1/4,-1/4,1)^\top$ vs. $\mu_{\text{skip}}=(1/2,-1/2,1)^\top$, $\cos = 0.962250$ in `exp58`, whereas applying the closed-form Stage-2 leverage inversion $\operatorname{diag}(1 - x_i^2/D)^{-1}$ prior to $P_x$ restores exact equality $\mathbb{E}[P_x\operatorname{diag}(1 - x_i^2/D)^{-1}\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}] = \mu_{\text{skip}}$ with $\cos = 1.000000$ in `exp60` Part 1 and yields `2.0519 ± 0.0009` vs. `2.0519 ± 0.0009` at 360st and `1.9632 ± 0.0009` vs. `1.9634 ± 0.0008` at 720st in `exp60` Arms 3 & 6; without inversion, its modulation-vector discrepancy is bounded by $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a\|_2}{\text{rms}(z)}$ and its outer-product matrix Frobenius discrepancy by $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a\|_2 \|h_n\|_2}{\text{rms}(z)}$, so the normalized discrepancy coefficient $\max_i x_i^2/D$ is $O(1/D)$ only under a coordinate-concentration assumption $\max_i x_i^2 = O(1)$, and need not converge with width if coordinates concentrate or if the scale factors $\|a\|_2, \|h_n\|_2$ are not controlled), (b) omitted inter-block vs. intra-block `attn -> mlp` paths (cf. [5]'s fixed-final-discrepancy `block_fixed_top_discrepancy_bp` and intra-block `MLP-Pullback` controls), (c) hidden-matrix optimizer-law matching, and (d) architectural `SSSLSL` window layout vs. terminal-skip path truncation in the `{Lower-Half, Upper-Half}` depth asymmetry, `exp57`, `exp58`, and `exp63` evaluate two distinct surrogate losses across three optimizer configurations and across depth subsets:
           1. **Sub-Block Direct-Skip Surrogate BP (`forward_terminal_skip_surrogate_loss`, `exp57`) vs. Block-Skip (Intra-Block `attn -> mlp` Preserved) Surrogate BP (`forward_block_skip_keep_intrablock_loss`, `exp58`):** In `forward_terminal_skip_surrogate_loss` (`exp57`), both block entry `curr` and post-attention `curr_after_attn = curr.detach() + attn_out.detach()` are detached before `norm`, removing **both** inter-block composition ($l' > l$) and intra-block attention-to-MLP credit (`attn -> mlp` within block $l$). In `forward_block_skip_keep_intrablock_loss` (`exp58`), `curr_in = curr.detach()` is detached only at block entry while `curr_after_attn = curr_in + attn_out` remains live into `block.mlp(norm(curr_after_attn))` (matching [5]'s fixed-final-discrepancy block-local formulation). At Seed 46 initialization ($D=384, G=2,048, M_g=1$), single-draw WM exhibits high empirical cosine with `g_subblock_skip` (`0.9982` on `wte`, `0.9996` on `attn.c_proj, mlp.c_proj`, `0.9992` on `mlp.c_fc`, `0.9997` on `attn.c_v`; split-half `0.9965–0.9994`), while $\cos(g_{\text{subblock\_skip}}, g_{\text{global}})$ and $\cos(g_{\text{block\_skip}}, g_{\text{global}})$ rise with block depth $l \in \{0, \dots, 5\}$ (from `0.9054 / 0.9050` in `0..2` to `0.9702 / 0.9697` in `3..5` for `g_subblock_skip` on $W_Q, W_K$, and reaching `1.0000` across all matrices at top block $l=5$ under `g_block_skip`; note that this single-seed initialization profile is compatible with greater skip fidelity near the head but does not by itself establish why the trained upper-half recipe retains its reported fraction of the gain). Across 3 seeds (`46, 47, 48`) under the shared-`lr=0.006` co-designed BP optimizer: in the **Writer-Only frozen-internals testbed** (`q, k, v, c_fc, value_embeds, ve_gate` frozen; `10.72M` active, `360` steps), Delayed WM (`2.0702 ± 0.0005` in `4.47s`), Sub-Block-Skip BP (`2.0690 ± 0.0005` in `2.94s`), and Global BP (`2.0633 ± 0.0003` in `5.62s`) lie within `0.0069` BPB. On **4-Matrix**, where `codesigned` (`wm_recipe_matched=False`) **already executes the exact 4-matrix `exp49` update law** (`run_exp49_d6_equal_compute_and_ve_gate.py`, lines 547–566: $b_t = \beta b_{t-1} + (1-\beta)g_t$, Nesterov $v_t = (1-\beta)g_t + \beta b_t$, `PolarExpress(v_t)`, weight decay `0.01`, and identical `lm_head`/`wte`/`ve` optimizers), Global BP $\to$ Block-Skip BP (`exp58`) $\to$ Sub-Block-Skip BP (`exp57`) $\to$ Delayed WM progresses as **`2.0532 ± 0.0008` $\to$ `2.0597 ± 0.0006` $\to$ `2.0600 ± 0.0006` $\to$ `2.0682 ± 0.0006`** at 360 steps, and **`1.9423 ± 0.0031` $\to$ `1.9813 ± 0.0005` $\to$ `1.9819 ± 0.0005` $\to$ `1.9989 ± 0.0007`** at 720 steps (where omitting inter-block paths costs `+0.0390` BPB, omitting intra-block `attn -> mlp` credit costs `+0.0006` BPB, and the exact-optimizer-matched difference between Sub-Block-Skip BP `1.9819` and 4-Matrix Delayed WM `1.9989` is **`+0.0170` BPB**). On **6-Matrix** under shared `lr=0.006`, Global BP $\to$ Block-Skip BP (`exp58`) $\to$ Sub-Block-Skip BP (`exp57`) progresses as **`2.0316 ± 0.0009` $\to$ `2.0458 ± 0.0003` $\to$ `2.0471 ± 0.0003`** at 360 steps, and **`1.8904 ± 0.0012` $\to$ `1.9334 ± 0.0009` $\to$ `1.9367 ± 0.0009`** at 720 steps (where intra-block `attn -> mlp` credit accounts for `+0.0013` and `+0.0033` BPB).
            2. **Exact `exp49` 6-Matrix Update-Law Controls (`exact_exp49_opt=True`, `exp58` Arms 9–12, `exp59` Arms 7–12, `exp67` Arms 6–7) vs. Alternative Weight-Row/Sphere-Projected EMA Baselines (`wm_recipe_matched=True`, `exp58` Arms 5–8, `exp59` Arms 1–4):** In `run_exp49_d6_equal_compute_and_ve_gate.py` (`lines 269–270, 547–560`) and `run_exp50_d6_equal_compute_qk_schedules.py`, the 6-matrix Delayed-WM optimizer updates each momentum buffer via $b_t = \beta b_{t-1} + (1-\beta)g_t$, passes the Nesterov mixture $v_t = (1-\beta)g_t + \beta b_t$ to `prepolar_muon_orthogonalize_batched(v_t, ns_steps=5)`, applies decoupled weight decay `p.mul_(1.0 - mlr * lrm * 0.01)` and step `p.add_(u_t, alpha=-(mlr * lrm))`, and for query/key matrices ($\eta_Q=0.048, \beta_Q=0.95, \eta_K \in \{0.012, 0.024\}, \beta_K=0.98$, `is_sph=True`) restores the Frobenius norm `p.mul_(init_norms[id(p)] / p.norm().clamp(min=1e-8))`. Running this **exact `exp49` 6-matrix update law** (`exact_exp49_opt=True`, `exp58` Arms 9–12, verified by source-correspondence and deterministic twin execution in `verify_paper5.py`) improves 360-step 6-Matrix Sub-Block-Skip BP (`subblock_skip_exact_exp49_opt_bp_d6_6mat_360steps`, Arm 9) from `2.0471` (shared `lr=0.006`) to **`2.0157 / 2.0159 / 2.0162` $\to$ `2.0159 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB (`1.9792 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `253.57` PPL, `9.72s`)**; and in `exp67` Arms 6–7, `exp68` Arms 4–9, `exp70` Arms 5–8, & `exp71` Arms 3–8, we evaluate the **complete matched `Per-Head QK` ($g_Q=g_K=1, g_V=6$) and `Per-Head QKV` ($g_Q=g_K=g_V=1$) 4-level surrogate hierarchy** at both $\eta_K=0.012$ and **matched $\eta_K=0.024$ (`muon_lr_k=0.024`)** across 360 and 720 steps (including `exp70` Arms 5–8: `block_skip_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qkv_{360,720}steps` at **`2.0054 ± 0.0007`** / **`1.8881 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB** and `subblock_skip_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qkv_{360,720}steps` at **`2.0073 ± 0.0003`** / **`1.8925 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB** at $\eta_K=0.012$; and `exp71` Arms 3–8 at **matched $\eta_K=0.024$**, yielding the unconfounded same-rate `Fixed-QK+V` progression **`1.9899 ± 0.0005` [Global BP] $\xrightarrow{+0.0133}$ `2.0032 ± 0.0009` [Block-Skip BP] $\xrightarrow{+0.0020}$ `2.0052 ± 0.0008` [Sub-Block-Skip BP] $\xrightarrow{+0.0391 / +0.0384}$ `2.0443 ± 0.0002` / `2.0436 ± 0.0002` [Delayed WM]** at 360 steps, and **`1.8530 ± 0.0009` [Global BP] $\xrightarrow{+0.0349}$ `1.8879 ± 0.0010` [Block-Skip BP] $\xrightarrow{+0.0041}$ `1.8920 ± 0.0007` [Sub-Block-Skip BP] $\xrightarrow{+0.0628 / +0.0623}$ `1.9548 ± 0.0004` / `1.9543 ± 0.0006` [Delayed WM]** at 720 steps): at **360 steps**, moving from Joint $(D, D)$ to Per-Head polar orthogonalization preserves the expected ordering across all four credit regimes under the specified recipe—**Global BP Per-Head QKV (`global_bp_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qkv_360steps`, `exp68` Arm 4: `1.9920 / 1.9915 / 1.9914` $\to$ `1.9916 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB, `1.9544 ± 0.0004` VAL-B, `237.17` PPL, `8.09s`) / Global BP Per-Head QK (`global_bp_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qk_360steps`, `exp67` Arm 6: `1.9934 / 1.9933 / 1.9936` $\to$ `1.9934 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB, `1.9563 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `238.37` PPL, `8.55s`) $\xrightarrow{+0.0134\text{ (inter-block)}}$ Block-Skip BP Per-Head QK (`block_skip_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qk_360steps`, `exp68` Arm 6: `2.0067 / 2.0062 / 2.0075` $\to$ `2.0068 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB, `1.9697 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `247.27` PPL, `7.15s`) $\xrightarrow{+0.0020\text{ (intra-block)}}$ Sub-Block-Skip BP Per-Head QK (`subblock_skip_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qk_360steps`, `exp68` Arm 8: `2.0084 / 2.0088 / 2.0091` $\to$ `2.0088 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB, `1.9717 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `248.62` PPL, `7.24s`, vs. `2.0159` Joint) $\xrightarrow{+0.0370\text{ (QK)} / +0.0348\text{ (QKV SVD)}}$ 6-Matrix Delayed WM $M=4$ (`2.0458` Per-Head QK, `2.0455` Per-Head QK SVD, `2.0443` Per-Head QKV, and `2.0436` Per-Head QKV SVD)**. At **720 steps**, Per-Head polar orthogonalization improves every credit engine by an invariant `-0.0069` to `-0.0089` BPB over Joint $g=6$, yielding both the Joint ($g=6$) progression (`1.8625 -> 1.8969 [+0.0344 inter-block] -> 1.9016 [+0.0047 intra-block] -> 1.9632 [+0.0616 WM]`) and the matched **Per-Head ($g=1$) 720-step progression**: **Global BP Per-Head QKV (`global_bp_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qkv_720steps`, `exp68` Arm 5: `1.8540 / 1.8543 / 1.8524` $\to$ `1.8536 ± 0.0010` DEV BPB, `1.8185 ± 0.0004` VAL-B, `162.34` PPL, `15.48s`) / Global BP Per-Head QK (`global_bp_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qk_720steps`, `exp67` Arm 7: `1.8549 / 1.8555 / 1.8542` $\to$ `1.8549 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB, `1.8199 ± 0.0007` VAL-B, `162.93` PPL, `17.28s`) $\xrightarrow{+0.0343\text{ (inter-block)}}$ Block-Skip BP Per-Head QK (`block_skip_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qk_720steps`, `exp68` Arm 7: `1.8892 / 1.8883 / 1.8901` $\to$ `1.8892 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB, `1.8544 ± 0.0010` VAL-B, `179.03` PPL, `14.41s`) $\xrightarrow{+0.0046\text{ (intra-block)}}$ Sub-Block-Skip BP Per-Head QK (`subblock_skip_exact_exp49_opt_d6_6mat_per_head_qk_720steps`, `exp68` Arm 9: `1.8941 / 1.8933 / 1.8941` $\to$ `1.8938 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB, `1.8589 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `181.32` PPL, `14.42s`) $\xrightarrow{+0.0627\text{ (QK)} / +0.0623\text{ (QK SVD)} / +0.0610\text{ (QKV)} / +0.0605\text{ (QKV SVD)}}$ 6-Matrix Delayed WM $M=4$ (`1.9565 ± 0.0004` Per-Head QK [`exp64`], `1.9561 ± 0.0008` Per-Head QK Exact SVD [`exp66`], `1.9548 ± 0.0004` Per-Head QKV [`exp68` Arm 1], and `1.9543 ± 0.0006` Per-Head QKV Exact SVD [`exp68` Arm 3])**. Separately, we also report `exp58` Arms 5–8 (`wm_recipe_matched=True`: `2.0205 ± 0.0002` on 360st 6-Mat Sub-Block-Skip; `1.9854 ± 0.0006` on 720st 4-Mat Sub-Block-Skip; `1.9128 ± 0.0009` on 720st 6-Mat Sub-Block-Skip; and `1.8731 ± 0.0007` on 720st 6-Mat Global BP) and `exp59` Arms 1–4 (`2.0548 ± 0.0007` and `2.0545 ± 0.0008` at 360st; `1.9733 ± 0.0014` and `1.9697 ± 0.0011` at 720st), which project core gradients onto weight rows, pass the non-Nesterov EMA buffer alone to `PolarExpress`, and project orthogonalized $Q/K$ updates onto the weight sphere's tangent plane before norm restoration; because these weight-space operations differ from `exp49`'s Nesterov update law, they are classified strictly as **alternative-optimizer baselines** (`1.8731 -> 1.9128 -> 1.9697` internal contrast) rather than `exp49`-optimizer-matched controls.
           3. **720-Step Credit-Engine Depth-Asymmetry Deconfounding: Architectural `SSSLSL` Window Layout (`~20%`) vs. Terminal-Skip Path Truncation (`~80%`, `exp63` Arms 4–7):** Does the `Upper-Half (3,4,5)` vs. `Lower-Half (0,1,2)` $(W_Q, W_K)$ utility asymmetry reflect the `SSSLSL` window schedule (where blocks `3, 5` are full-context `L`) or terminal-skip path truncation? In `exp63` (`Arms 4–7`, `exact_exp49_opt=True`, `720` steps), we evaluate `Lower-Half (0,1,2)` vs. `Upper-Half (3,4,5)` active $(W_Q, W_K)$ under **both** Exact Global Backpropagation and Sub-Block Direct-Skip Surrogate Backpropagation:
              - **Under Exact Global Backpropagation (`4-Mat 1.9423 -> 6-Mat 1.8625`, `-0.0798` BPB full 6-block gain):** Activating `Lower-Half (0,1,2)` $(W_Q, W_K)$ (`global_bp_exact_exp49_opt_d6_lower_half_012_qk_720steps`, Arm 4) reaches **`1.8789 / 1.8778 / 1.8803` $\to$ `1.8790 ± 0.0013` DEV BPB (`1.8439 ± 0.0012` VAL-B, `174.08` PPL, `13.68s`, `-0.0633` BPB = `79.3%` of the 6-block gain)**, whereas activating `Upper-Half (3,4,5)` $(W_Q, W_K)$ (`global_bp_exact_exp49_opt_d6_upper_half_345_qk_720steps`, Arm 5) reaches **`1.8756 / 1.8761 / 1.8760` $\to$ `1.8759 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB (`1.8408 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `172.62` PPL, `13.94s`, `-0.0664` BPB = `83.2%` of the 6-block gain)**. Thus, under full inter-block backpropagation, the architectural `Upper-Half vs. Lower-Half` gap is only **`-0.0031` DEV BPB** (`3.9` percentage points of retention, `83.2% vs. 79.3%`)!
              - **Under Sub-Block Direct-Skip Surrogate Backpropagation (`4-Mat 1.9819 -> 6-Mat 1.9016`, `-0.0803` BPB full 6-block gain):** Activating `Lower-Half (0,1,2)` $(W_Q, W_K)$ (`subblock_skip_exact_exp49_opt_bp_d6_lower_half_012_qk_720steps`, Arm 6) reaches **`1.9239 / 1.9222 / 1.9230` $\to$ `1.9230 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (`1.8880 ± 0.0007` VAL-B, `196.47` PPL, `11.28s`, `-0.0589` BPB = `73.3%` of the 6-block gain)**, whereas activating `Upper-Half (3,4,5)` $(W_Q, W_K)$ (`subblock_skip_exact_exp49_opt_bp_d6_upper_half_345_qk_720steps`, Arm 7) reaches **`1.9070 / 1.9077 / 1.9079` $\to$ `1.9075 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`1.8727 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `188.29` PPL, `11.21s`, `-0.0744` BPB = `92.7%` of the 6-block gain)**—magnifying the `Upper-Half vs. Lower-Half` gap **$5.0\times$ from `-0.0031` BPB to `-0.0155` DEV BPB** (`19.3` percentage points of retention, `92.7% vs. 73.3%`), which closely mirrors Delayed WM's `90.1%–94.4% vs. 63.1%–67.4%` retention (`-0.0053` to `-0.0073` BPB gap out of `-0.0233` BPB). As a recipe-specific descriptive contrast between these two 720-step training regimes (at absolute losses `~1.876–1.879` under Global BP vs. `~1.908–1.923` under Sub-Block Direct-Skip BP, rather than an exact additive structural decomposition), the Global-BP `Upper-Half vs. Lower-Half` gap (`-0.0031` BPB) is **`20.0%` (`-0.0031 / -0.0155`)** of the Sub-Block-Skip gap (`-0.0155` BPB), while the remaining **`80.0%` (`-0.0124 / -0.0155`)** arises when inter-block and intra-block `attn -> mlp` paths are detached (because lower-block attention routing relies heavily on composing through intermediate blocks $l' > l$, whereas upper-block attention routing acts directly on the terminal skip bus).

**Table 5:** 3-seed optimizer co-design, factorized component ablations, 5-matrix Active-$W_Q/W_K$ routing (`exp45_d6_attn_routing_results.json`), full 6-matrix (`26,345,472 / 26,346,060 = 99.9978%` active parameters) joint $(W_Q, W_K)$ routing + $M \in \{2, 4\}$ amortized multi-probe ablations (`exp47`, `exp49`, `exp53`, `exp56`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp63`, `exp64`, `exp66`–`exp76`), unconfounded $\eta_K=0.024$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ controls (`exp63`), closed-form parameter-free `Wiener-MMSE`, Sparse `Top-K=32`, Walsh-Hadamard $H_4$ Temporal Multiplexing, Stage-2 `RMSNorm` leverage inversion, causal attention-weighted `Rake` key gather (`Proposition 6`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp62`), groupwise/headwise polar granularity $g \in \{1, 2, 3, 6\}$ on $(W_Q, W_K)$ and $g_V=1$ on $W_V$ (`Fixed-QK+V`) with Rectangular Stiefel `PolarExpress` (`Proposition 7`, Zhang et al. [12], `exp64`, `exp67`–`exp71`), energy-matched linear spectral tapering (`PrePolar-Blend` & Exact-SVD Taper $\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `Proposition 8`, `exp72`, `exp73`, `exp75`), tier-asymmetric linear tapering, Gavish-Donoho [13] optimal singular-value shrinkage $\eta^*(y)$, and un-equilibrated cubic Gram spectral tapering (`Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` & `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `Proposition 9`, `exp76`), `mlp.c_fc` $k_{\text{mlp}}=4$ row-partitioning and $\gamma_{V,\text{mlp}}=0.10$ controls (`exp72`, `exp75`), 3-component `Per-Head Pre-Polar Muon` vs. `Exact FP32 SVD Polar` + `Global/Per-Head Sphere` deconfounding controls (`exp66`, `exp67`, `exp71`), 720-step `both_stages_pre_install` clock controls (`exp59`), exploratory active-`ve_gate` vs. pure $M=4$ controls (`26,346,048 = 100%` of matrix weights active), main-RNG-matched and layer-RNG-synchronized 720-step $2 \times 2$ `{Lower-Half, Upper-Half}` depth factorials (`exp51`, `exp53`, `exp56`), 720-step credit-engine depth-asymmetry deconfounding across Global BP and Sub-Block-Skip BP (`exp63`), fixed-probe Upper-Half $M=4$ reallocation (`exp53`, `exp56`, `exp59`), Stage-2 compositional/bus controls (`exp53`, `exp54`), Writer-Only, Block-Skip (`attn -> mlp` preserved), Sub-Block Direct-Skip, Exact-`exp49`-Optimizer (`exact_exp49_opt`), Global BP Per-Head QK/QKV and `PrePolar-Blend` / `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` double-dissociation controls at 360 and 720 steps (`exp67`, `exp68`, `exp70`, `exp71`, `exp73`, `exp75`, `exp76`), Alternative Weight-Row/Sphere-Projected EMA Surrogate/Global BP controls (`exp57`, `exp58`), and same-trainer matched-training-region-time controls (`384, 438, 720, 740, 840, 875, 1,004` steps in `exp49`–`exp51`, `exp53`–`exp54`, `exp62`, `exp64`, `exp70`, `exp73`, `exp76`) on ClimbMix `d6`, reporting measured 3-seed training-region GPU time (`Run GPU`, forward + update on an uncontended RTX 4090) and 30-step steady-state benchmark estimates (`Est.`).

### 4.7 Comparison with Surrogate-Routed (`SR-LNP`) and Suffix-Recomputed (`SR-NP`) Node Perturbation Across Tokens, Training-Region Time, and Width $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$ (`exp37`, `exp39`, `exp42`, `exp49`–`exp51`, `exp53`–`exp54`, `exp56`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp62`–`exp64`, `exp66`–`exp73`, `exp75`–`exp76`)

Table 6 reports our 3-seed comparison between **Fused Triton Delayed WM** (across 4-matrix, Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$, and Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ under $\gamma_K = 0.85$, closed-form `wiener_mmse + hadamard4`, **Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel `PolarExpress` + `wiener_mmse + hadamard4`**, **Per-Head Exact FP32 SVD Polar Factor [no 2-pass equilibration] + `wiener_mmse + hadamard4`**, **Energy-Matched Linear Spectral Tapering [`PrePolar-Blend` & `Exact-SVD Taper` $\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, Proposition 8]**, and **Gavish-Donoho [13] Optimal Shrinkage $\eta^*(y)$ & Un-Equilibrated Cubic Gram Spectral Tapering [`Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` & `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, Proposition 9]**), **Surrogate-Routed Local Node Perturbation (`SR-LNP`, $K \in \{4, 16, 64\}$)**, and **Suffix-Recomputed Node Perturbation (`SR-NP`, $K_{\text{site}}=4, K_{\text{tot}}=76$ suffix-recomputed block draws $+ 24$ local attention draws/step, $\gamma = 0.98$, `exp42`)** across equal token budgets ($360$ steps $= 0.737\text{M}$ tokens), matched training-region GPU time ($500$ steps $= 9.41\text{s}$ run GPU matching `SR-LNP K=16`'s $9.43\text{s}$ in `exp39`, and $720$–$1,004$ steps $= 13.57\text{s}$–$128.55\text{s}$ run GPU vs. `SR-LNP K=64`'s $23.66\text{s}$ across `exp39`, `exp49`–`exp51`, `exp53`–`exp54`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp62`, `exp64`, `exp66`–`exp68`, `exp70`, `exp72`, `exp73`, and `exp76`), and model widths $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$. We make no claim of superiority over published Dust [9]:
1. **Equal-Token Comparison on `d6` ($360$ Steps, $D=384$):** `wm_G2048_codesigned_opt_triton` (`2.0682 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB in `6.74s` run GPU / `6.72s` est.) outperforms `SR-LNP (K=4)` (`2.3071 ± 0.0016` in `6.32s` / `5.48s` est.) by **`-0.2389` BPB (`-48.1%` PPL)**, `SR-LNP (K=16)` (`2.2603 ± 0.0018` in `9.43s` / `9.23s` est.) by **`-0.1921` BPB (`-41.0%` PPL) in `1.40x` less run GPU time**, `SR-LNP (K=64)` (`2.2041 ± 0.0013` in `23.66s` / `23.22s` est.) by **`-0.1359` BPB (`-31.2%` PPL) in `3.51x` less run GPU time**, and Suffix-Recomputed `SR-NP (K_tot=76)` under `Adam` (`2.4264 ± 0.0088` in `84.38s`) and `Pre-Polar Muon` (`2.2415 ± 0.0015` in `65.19s`) by **`-0.3582` BPB** and **`-0.1733` BPB in `9.67x` less GPU time**. Even at $G=64$ (`5.79s` run GPU / `5.70s` est.), `wm_G64_codesigned_opt_triton` (`2.2415 ± 0.0011`) matches `SR-NP (K_tot=76, Muon)` in $11.3\times$ less GPU time.
2. **Matched Training-Region Time & 720–1,004 Step Horizon Comparison (`9.41s` to `128.55s` Run GPU on `d6`, $D=384$):** In the specialized 4-matrix `exp39` trainer (`~18.8 ms/step`), at matched training-region time to `SR-LNP (K=16)` (`9.41s` vs. `9.43s` run GPU; `9.34s` vs. `9.23s` est., `500` steps), `wm_G2048_codesigned_500steps_match_dustK16_time` reaches **`2.0322 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9975 ± 0.0006` VAL-B BPB, `265.13` PPL)**—outperforming `SR-LNP (K=16)` by **`-0.2281` BPB (`-46.6%` PPL)**—and at `720` steps (`13.57s` run GPU / `13.45s` est., `1.74x` faster than `SR-LNP K=64` and `4.80x` faster than `SR-NP K_tot=76`), `wm_G2048_codesigned_720steps_half_dustK64_time` breaks the `2.00` DEV BPB barrier backward-free at **`1.9989 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB (`1.9646 ± 0.0008` VAL-B BPB, `241.96` PPL)**. In the general 6-matrix `exp49`–`exp76` trainer (Table 5), 720-step Full 6-Matrix $M=2$ (`21.67s` / `21.77s`) reaches **`1.9763 ± 0.0009` / `1.9756 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB**, 720-step Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$ reaches **`1.9699 ± 0.0001` DEV BPB (`21.05s`, $\gamma_K=0.85$)** and **`1.9672 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB (`22.36s`, `wiener_mmse`)**, 740-step Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$ (`19.19s`) reaches **`1.9670 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB**, 720-step Pure Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ reaches **`1.9672 ± 0.0005` (`27.71s`, $\gamma_K=0.85, \eta_K=0.012$)**, **`1.9671 ± 0.0006` (`23.29s`, $\gamma_K=0.85, \eta_K=0.024$)**, **`1.9633 ± 0.0009` (`24.36s`, sparse `wiener_mmse_topk32`)**, **`1.9632 ± 0.0006` / `1.9632 ± 0.0009` (`29.14s` / `24.54s`, dense `wiener_mmse` and `hadamard4 + lev_inv`)**, **`1.9565 ± 0.0004` / `1.9566 ± 0.0002` / `1.9566 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`1.9214` / `1.9216` VAL-B, `215.39` / `215.43` / `215.46` PPL, `23.79s` / `23.68s` / `28.96s`, Per-Head Stiefel `PolarExpress` + `wiener_mmse` + `hadamard4` / `topk32` / `global_sphere`, `exp64` Arms 4 & 5, `exp66` Arm 5)**, **`1.9561 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB (`1.9210 ± 0.0006` VAL-B, `215.15` PPL, `107.33s`, Per-Head Exact FP32 SVD Polar Factor [no 2-pass equilibration] + Global Sphere, `exp66` Arm 6)**, **`1.9548 ± 0.0004` / `1.9543 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`1.9198` / `1.9192` VAL-B, `214.38` / `214.08` PPL, `23.86s` / `127.30s`, `Fixed-QK+V` `Pre-Polar` / `Exact SVD`, `exp68` Arms 1 & 3)**, **`1.9476 ± 0.0008` / `1.9465 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.9124 ± 0.0007` / `1.9115 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `210.21` / `209.56` PPL, `23.78s` / `128.55s`, [13] Optimal Shrinker $\eta^*(y)$, `exp76` Arms 8, 10, & 12—surpassing 720-step 4-Matrix Exact Global Backpropagation `1.9423 ± 0.0031` at equal tokens and recovering `111.9%` / `112.1%` / `113.3%` of the shared-LR 4-to-6-matrix Global-BP gain `1.9423 -> 1.8904` and `64.6%` / `64.7%` / `65.4%` of the matched `PrePolar-Blend` Global-BP gain `1.9423 -> 1.8518`)**; and at the `~19.7–22.9s` same-trainer matched training-region time budget (`1.03–1.20x` faster than `SR-LNP K=64`'s `23.66s`), 840-step Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=2$ reaches **`1.9609 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`21.71s`, `exp73` Arm 3)**, and **`1.9337 ± 0.0015` DEV BPB (`1.8990 ± 0.0014` VAL-B, `202.29` PPL, `22.90s`, `Fixed-QK+V` + `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `exp76` Arm 13)**—outperforming 1,004-step 4-matrix WM (`1.9720 ± 0.0006` in `21.55s`) by **`-0.0300` to `-0.0383` DEV BPB** and `SR-LNP K=64` by **`-0.2621` to `-0.2704` BPB (`-51.3%` to `-52.4%` PPL)**.
3. **Model Width Scaling ($D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$ at Fixed Head Dimension $d_h = 64$), Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel `PolarExpress`, Linear Spectral Tapering, and Un-Equilibrated Cubic Gram Spectral Tapering (`Propositions 7–9`, `exp39`, `exp56`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp62`–`exp64`, `exp66`–`exp73`, `exp75`–`exp76`):** Because width $D = H \cdot d_h$ scales by increasing the number of attention heads $H \in \{4, 6, 8, 12\}$ while keeping per-head dimension $d_h = 64$ invariant, the coupled per-head query/key subspace dimension $m_{\text{eff}} = d_h = 64$ remains constant while the aspect ratio $\beta = d_h / D = 1/H$ shrinks from $1/4 \to 1/12$ and the $1/(MD)$ prefactor in Stage-2 terminal `RMSNorm` cross-coordinate variance (Proposition 1c) scales inversely with $D$ at a fixed moment ratio (though across trained models the activation $x_n$ and sensitivity $a_n$ distributions also vary with width, so a literal $3\times$ variance reduction cannot be inferred from $D: 256 \to 768$ alone; empirically, `exp56` Part 1 measures a decreasing average $x_i^2/D$ prefactor at initialization). Consequently:
   - **4-Matrix Width Scaling (`exp39`):** As width $D$ grows from `256 -> 384 -> 512 -> 768` (`14.55M -> 67.11M` active parameters), 4-matrix `Delayed WM`'s gap to 4-matrix Exact Backpropagation **shrinks from `+0.0144` BPB to `+0.0125` BPB** (`2.0933` vs. `2.0789` at $D=256$; `2.0558 ± 0.0002` vs. `2.0433 ± 0.0006` at $D=768$), while 4-matrix `Delayed WM` at the smallest width $D=256$ (`2.0933 ± 0.0004` in `5.03s` run GPU) outperforms `SR-LNP (K=64)` at the largest width $D=768$ (`2.1729 ± 0.0004` in `95.74s` run GPU) by **`-0.0796` BPB**.
   - **Joint $(D,D)$ vs. Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel $(H, d_h, D)$ Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ Width Scaling, Linear `PrePolar-Blend`, & Cubic `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` (`Propositions 7–9`, `exp49`, `exp56`, `exp59`, `exp60`, `exp62`–`exp64`, `exp66`–`exp73`, `exp75`–`exp76`):** Under whole-matrix $(D, D)$ `PolarExpress`, Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ ($\gamma_K=0.85$ in `exp56`, `2.0794 ± 0.0009, 2.0552 ± 0.0007, 2.0451 ± 0.0005, 2.0437 ± 0.0006`; and `wiener_mmse + hadamard4` in `exp60`/`exp62` Arms 1–3, **`2.0768 ± 0.0010` in `11.11s` at $D=256$, `2.0519 ± 0.0009` in `12.20s` at $D=384$, `2.0427 ± 0.0007` in `15.99s` at $D=512$, and `2.0418 ± 0.0002` in `29.92s` at $D=768$**) exhibits a **declining 4-to-6-matrix Exact-BP recovery fraction as $H = D/64$ grows**: `69.8%, 60.2%, 53.4%, 43.2%` under $\gamma_K=0.85$ and **`82.9%, 75.5%, 62.9%, 50.0%` under `wiener_mmse + hadamard4`**. Simultaneously, Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$ ($\gamma_K=0.85$: `2.0814, 2.0562, 2.0467, 2.0454`) captures `85.6%–92.3%` of the joint 6-block gain at half the SDPA probes. Why does whole-matrix $(D, D)$ `PolarExpress` lose efficiency as $H = D/64$ increases? As proved in **Proposition 7** (`Section 3.4`, Item 5) and verified in `exp64` Part 1 (synthetic shared-input rank-$d_h$ audit) and `exp66` Part 1B (real ClimbMix `d6` Step-0 and Step-120 checkpoint audit across all 6 blocks), orthogonalizing $W_Q, W_K$ as a single $(D, D)$ matrix mixes the $H$ independent attention heads inside $G_t^\top G_t = \sum_{h=1}^H G_{t,h}^\top G_{t,h}$, summing $H$ independent probe-noise sample Gram terms and imposing $H(H-1)d_h^2/2$ spurious cross-head orthogonality constraints ($24,576 \to 270,336$ constraints as $H: 4 \to 12$). Reshaping each $W_Q, W_K$ update into **`(H, d_h, D)` (`Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel PolarExpress`, `exp63` Arm 8 & `exp64` Arms 1–3; `Per-Head Exact FP32 SVD Polar Factor`, `exp66` Arms 4 & 6; linear `PrePolar-Blend` $\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `exp72` Arm 1 & `exp75` Arms 1–3; and un-equilibrated cubic `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `exp76` Arms 9 & 14–16)** preserves exact total update energy $\|U_{\text{exact}}^{\text{head}}\|_F^2 = D$ while **monotonically increasing both the per-head improvement and the spectral-tapering gain (`-0.0086, -0.0106, -0.0111, -0.0136` BPB for `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` over `Fixed-QK+V`) as $H \in \{4, 6, 8, 12\}$ grows**:
     - **$D=256$ ($H=4$, `exp64` Arm 1, `exp70` Arm 1, `exp75` Arm 1, & `exp76` Arm 14):** **`2.0731 ± 0.0014` DEV BPB (`11.04s`)** under `Fixed-QK`, **`2.0726 ± 0.0013` DEV BPB (`2.0352` VAL-B, `296.21` PPL, `11.09s`, `99.0%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP)** under `Fixed-QK+V`, **`2.0675 ± 0.0017` DEV BPB (`2.0299` VAL-B, `292.08` PPL, `11.88s`, `129.6%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP)** under `PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$), and **`2.0644 / 2.0632 / 2.0644` $\to$ `2.0640 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`2.0264 ± 0.0005` VAL-B, `289.34` PPL, `11.71s`, `-0.0149` BPB vs. 4-Mat BP `2.0789`, `147.2%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP `2.0590`)** under `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$)!
     - **$D=384$ ($H=6$, `exp64` Arm 2, `exp66` Arm 4, `exp67`/`exp68`, `exp72` Arm 1, `exp73` Arm 1, & `exp76` Arms 3, 9, & 11):** **`2.0458 ± 0.0003` (`11.90s`)** / **`2.0455 ± 0.0001` (`48.89s`)** under `Fixed-QK` Pre-Polar / Exact SVD, **`2.0443 ± 0.0002` (`14.48s`)** / **`2.0436 ± 0.0002` (`63.56s`)** under `Fixed-QK+V`, **`2.0382 ± 0.0003` (`11.86s`)** / **`2.0377 ± 0.0003` (`63.99s`)** under linear `PrePolar-Blend` / `Exact-SVD Taper` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$), and **`2.0337 ± 0.0017` (`48.96s`, `Gavish-Donoho` [13] Optimal Shrinker $\eta^*(y)$)**, **`2.0335 / 2.0334 / 2.0342` $\to$ `2.0337 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB (`1.9961 ± 0.0003` VAL-B, `266.22` PPL, `14.49s`, `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$)**, and **`2.0334 / 2.0337 / 2.0341` $\to$ `2.0337 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB (`1.9962 ± 0.0002` VAL-B, `266.28` PPL, `48.86s`, `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$)**—beating 4-Matrix Exact BP (`2.0532`) by **`-0.0195` BPB** and recovering **`159.7%`** of the shared-LR 4-to-6-matrix Exact-BP gain (`2.0532 -> 2.0316`; **`54.7%`** of `Per-Head Global-BP` `2.0532 -> 1.9879/1.9899` and **`65.9%`** of `Per-Head Sub-Block Surrogate-BP` `2.0600 -> 2.0039/2.0052`)!
     - **$D=512$ ($H=8$, `exp64` Arm 3, `exp70` Arm 2, `exp75` Arm 2, & `exp76` Arm 15):** **`2.0344 ± 0.0005` (`15.77s`)** under `Fixed-QK`, **`2.0317 ± 0.0004` (`15.49s`, `106.8%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP)** under `Fixed-QK+V`, **`2.0259 ± 0.0002` (`1.9887` VAL-B, `260.60` PPL, `19.03s`, `129.9%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP)** under `PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$), and **`2.0205 / 2.0219 / 2.0193` $\to$ `2.0206 ± 0.0013` DEV BPB (`1.9832 ± 0.0012` VAL-B, `256.84` PPL, `18.92s`, `-0.0236` BPB vs. 4-Mat BP `2.0442`, `151.0%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP `2.0191`)** under `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$)!
     - **$D=768$ ($H=12$, `exp63` Arm 8, `exp70` Arm 3, `exp75` Arm 3, & `exp76` Arm 16):** **`2.0313 ± 0.0009` (`29.47s`)** under `Fixed-QK`, **`2.0274 ± 0.0011` (`28.58s`, `101.4%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP)** under `Fixed-QK+V`, **`2.0207 ± 0.0008` (`1.9835` VAL-B, `256.86` PPL, `35.26s`, `125.4%` of shared-LR 6-Mat BP)** under `PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$), and **`2.0137 / 2.0140 / 2.0136` $\to$ `2.0138 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB (`1.9763 ± 0.0003` VAL-B, `252.05` PPL, `35.12s`, `-0.0295` BPB vs. 4-Mat BP `2.0433`)** under `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$)—surpassing even the shared-`lr=0.006` Joint 6-Matrix Exact-BP baseline (`2.0153 ± 0.0005`) by **`-0.0015` DEV BPB (`150.0%` recovery)** while still trailing matched Per-Head 6-Matrix Global BP (cf. `1.9899` at $D=384$).

Note: `width*_backprop_6mat_codesigned` (`2.0590, 2.0316, 2.0191, 2.0153`) uses shared `lr=0.006` Joint Muon (`exp56`), whereas at $D=384$ matched-optimizer Per-Head Global BP scores `1.9899` / `1.9879` / `1.9929` (Table 5). Both measured 3-seed training GPU time (`Run GPU`) and 30-step steady-state benchmark estimates (`Est.`) are reported.

**Table 7:** Controlled 3-seed Native `50,332,176` long-context ($T=2,048$, 100 steps $= 13.1\text{M}$ tokens) results across Delayed Installation (`exp29`), True Both-Stages Pre-Install (`exp30`), Strict Post-Installation Construction (`exp32`), Fused Triton Strict Post-Install (`exp40`, `exp41`), Native `50.3M` `SR-LNP K=16` (`exp41`), our $2 \times 2$ `{M_g = 8, M_g = 1} x {lr_embed = 3e-3, lr_embed = 0.015}` GPU-Resident Fused-Triton factorial (`exp43_native50m_master_results.json`), our `scale_factor = 8.0` and $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ Pinned-Ring-Buffer controls (`exp44_native50m_controls_results.json`, `9.61–9.69 GiB` peak VRAM), our 3-seed 5-matrix Spherical Active-$W_Q$ transfer (`exp46_native50m_active_q_results.json`, `9.80 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters), our 3-seed Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ transfer at $M=2$ and $M=4$ (`exp55_native50m_upper_half_qk_results.json`, `10.09 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters), our 3-seed full 6-matrix (`50,331,648 / 50,332,176 = 99.999%` active parameters) Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ transfer at $M=1$ and $M=2$ (`exp48_native50m_6mat_active_qk_results.json`, `10.08–10.24 GiB` peak VRAM), our 3-seed Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` closed-form `Wiener-MMSE` ($M=2$) and `Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4` ($M=4$) transfer (`exp61_native50m_wiener_mmse_key_gather_results.json`, `9.96–10.06 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters), our 3-seed unconfounded $\eta_K=0.024$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ controls and **Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel `PolarExpress` (`Proposition 7`)** transfer (`exp65_native50m_per_head_stiefel_and_lrk024_controls_results.json` & `exp69_native50m_per_head_qkv_results.json`, `10.20–10.36 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters), and our 3-seed **Un-Equilibrated `Exact-SVD Polar` & `Exact-SVD Taper` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `exp75`), Equilibrated `PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `exp74`), and Un-Equilibrated `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `exp76`)** transfers (`10.24–10.36 GiB` peak VRAM across `48.23M` active parameters). Note: all timing columns report 3-seed means; in `exp29`–`exp32`, the third timing column reports `t_wall_step_ms_including_eval_mean`, whereas in `exp40`–`exp48`, `exp55`, `exp61`, `exp65`, `exp69`, and `exp74`–`exp76` it reports `Fwd + Rule` (`t_total_step_gpu_ms`); matched `Fwd + Rule` comparisons use `1,425.01 + 6,432.04 = 7,857.05 ms` for `exp32`.

**Genuinely Isolated Kernel Substitution (`benchmark_native50m_isolated_kernel.json`) vs. `exp43` Implementation-Package Speedup, $2 \times 2$ `{M_g, lr_embed}` Factorial, `exp44` Controls, and `exp46`/`exp48`/`exp55`/`exp61`/`exp65`/`exp69`/`exp74`–`exp76` Native 5/6-Matrix, Upper-Half Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$, Per-Head Stiefel, `Exact-SVD Polar`/`Taper`, `PrePolar-Blend`, & `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` Scale-Transfer:**
1. **Separating the `exp43` Combined Implementation Package from Genuinely Isolated Kernel Substitutions:**
   In `exp40` (`1.9845 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB at `lr_wte = 0.10`) and `exp41` (`2.0252 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB at `lr_wte = 0.015`), we had changed the `wte` credit path and optimizer schedule (replacing `exp32`'s Stage-3 `lm_head` lexical contraction on `dfwd_x_final_wte` with the diagonal skip surrogate and applying `lrm -> 0.2, eps = 1e-4` to `lm_head`). In `exp43` (`native50m_strict_post_install_M8_exp32_exact_triton`), we preserve `exp32`'s exact Stage-3 lexical readout and optimizer schedule (`eps = 1e-8`, constant AdamW LR, `lrm: 1.0 -> 0.5` on Muon), scoring **`1.9359 / 1.9356 / 1.9360` $\to$ `1.9358 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB (`1.9205 ± 0.0002` VAL-B BPB, `231.97` PPL)** and matching `exp32` (`1.9361 ± 0.0004`) to within `0.0003` BPB.
   However, `run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py` differs from `exp32_strict_post_install_construction.py` in several systems and precision components beyond the WM contraction kernel: `exp32` passes `FP32` `q_r, k_r, v` to SDPA (`fa3.flash_attn_func`), computes `FP32` attention-filtered traces during the forward pass, offloads delayed traces to unpinned CPU memory, orthogonalizes hidden-matrix updates individually, and uses a dense one-hot target at `lm_head` (**`9.11 GiB` peak VRAM**, **`1,425.01 + 6,432.04 = 7,857.05 ms/step` on matched `fwd + rule` timed regions**, and `8,070.22 ms` `t_wall_step_ms_including_eval_mean`), whereas `exp43` casts `q_r, k_r, v` to `BF16` before SDPA (`run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py:243-248`), keeps delayed traces on GPU with pinned-memory ring buffers only for the 4D MLP activations (`pin_h_mlp, pin_dfwd_h`), reconstructs Stage-1 attention-filtered inputs at consumption time from `BF16` cached `q_rs, k_rs, norm_attn_ins` (`run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py:159-161`), batches `Pre-Polar Muon` orthogonalization across layers, uses an in-place scatter at `lm_head`, and replaces `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast` with fused Triton kernels (**`20.57 GiB` peak VRAM**, **`1,332.65 + 1,659.19 = 2,991.84 ms/step` on matched `fwd + rule` timed regions**). Thus, comparing `exp32` to `exp43` measures a **combined implementation package** that achieves a **`2.63x` speedup on matched `fwd + rule` timed regions (`7,857.05 -> 2,991.84 ms/step`, and `3.88x` on the learning-rule region `6,432.04 -> 1,659.19 ms`)** in exchange for **`+11.46 GiB` higher peak GPU memory (`9.11 -> 20.57 GiB`)**—or **`9.61–9.69 GiB` peak VRAM (`4,872.5` and `5,371.7 ms/step` 3-seed mean `fwd + rule` in Table 7)** when all 8 delayed trace tensors (`pin_h_mlp, pin_y_attn, pin_s_lm, pin_dfwd_h, pin_norm_mlp, pin_norm_attn, pin_q_r, pin_k_r`) are placed in pinned host ring buffers (`run_exp44_native50m_controls.py`).
   To establish the **genuinely isolated effect of the fused Triton contraction kernels** with all storage, precision, trace construction, optimizer execution, head calculation, and timing boundaries held 100% identical, we execute three isolated benchmarks in `benchmark_native50m_isolated_kernel.py` (`benchmark_native50m_isolated_kernel.json`, where the 3 steady-state samples measure within-run steady-state variation):
   - **Standalone 288-Call/Step Kernel Microbenchmark (`Part 1`, `2` warmup + `10` timed iterations, or `4` timed iterations for $M_g=1$ PyTorch):** `exp32`'s PyTorch baseline (`compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast`) processes stream chunks (`chunk_G = 256` by default, allocating **`4,618.5–4,632.5 MB`** of temporary 3D chunk workspace; or `chunk_G = 32`, allocating **`618.1–632.1 MB`**). Across the 18 WM contractions per microbatch (`288` calls/step), replacing `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast` with `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` (`0.0 MB` 3D workspace; only the `8.0 MB` or `64.0 MB` output buffer) speeds up the 288-call suite **`8.35x` vs. `chunk_G=256` (`2,749.29 ± 5.56 -> 329.41 ± 0.90 ms/step`) and `7.15x` vs. `chunk_G=32` (`2,353.90 ± 1.65 -> 329.41 ± 0.90 ms/step`)** at $M_g=8$ (`lm_head_cos = 0.99999733, rel_l2 = 2.34e-3`), and **`36.74x` vs. `chunk_G=256` (`21,437.38 ± 14.37 -> 583.45 ± 0.89 ms/step`) and `31.31x` vs. `chunk_G=32` (`18,270.57 ± 12.66 -> 583.45 ± 0.89 ms/step`)** at $M_g=1$ (`lm_head_cos = 0.99999994, rel_l2 = 1.52e-7`).
   - **Isolated Substitution Inside `exp32`'s Exact CPU-Offloaded Trainer (`Part 2`, $M_g=8$, `3`-step CPU-heap warmup followed by `5` steps `0–4` including pipeline startup `steps 0–1` and `3` steady-state steps `2–4`):** Swapping *only* `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast(chunk_G=256)` $\to$ `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` inside `exp32_strict_post_install_construction.py`'s exact CPU-offloaded trainer reduces **steady-state (`steps 2–4`, where all 288 calls/step are active) learning-rule time `1.60x` (`6,456.78 ± 0.64 -> 4,039.89 ± 0.20 ms/step`, saving `2,416.89 ms/step`—matching Part 1's `2,419.88 ms/step` kernel savings to within `3.0 ms`) and steady-state `fwd + rule` step time `1.44x` (`7,876.97 ± 0.65 -> 5,460.10 ± 0.52 ms/step`)**; averaged across all `5` steps `0–4` including pipeline startup (`step 0: 1,841.2 vs. 1,840.7 ms` rule; `step 1: 3,764.9 vs. 2,683.6 ms` rule), it reduces rule time **`1.50x` (`4,995.29 ± 2,113.64 -> 3,328.78 ± 1,018.30 ms/step`)** and `fwd + rule` time **`1.35x` (`6,415.55 ± 2,113.31 -> 4,749.72 ± 1,016.99 ms/step`)**, while **reducing peak VRAM from `9.108 GiB` to `7.629 GiB` (`-1.479 GiB`)** and matching step-5 DEV BPB identically (`2.6686` vs. `2.6686`).
   - **Isolated Substitution Inside `exp43`'s Exact GPU-Resident Trainer (`Part 3`, $M_g=8$ and $M_g=1$, `2` warmup steps `0–1` + `3` timed steady-state steps `2–4` with per-contraction `cuda.synchronize()` around `contract_fn`):** Inside `run_exp43_native50m_mg1.py`'s GPU-resident trainer (`19.50 GiB` base allocation), `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast` with `chunk_G=256` (`4.62 GB` temp HBM) exceeds the `23.55 GiB` capacity of an RTX 4090 (`OOM`), requiring `chunk_G=32` (`~618 MB` temp HBM) to fit on GPU. Holding `exp43`'s GPU-resident storage, pinned MLP ring buffers, `BF16` SDPA and deferred Stage-1 attention-filtered input reconstruction, batched Muon orthogonalization, in-place scatter head calculation, and timing boundaries 100% identical (`20.566 GiB` peak VRAM at $M_g=8$; `20.646 GiB` at $M_g=1$), swapping *only* `compute_stream_wm_rewards_fast(chunk_G=32)` $\to$ `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` speeds up the 288-call contraction **`6.86x` (`2,363.30 ± 0.72 -> 344.60 ± 0.74 ms`)**, the learning rule **`2.19x` (`3,725.38 ± 0.69 -> 1,702.87 ± 0.98 ms`)**, and `fwd + rule` step time **`1.67x` (`5,055.97 ± 1.30 -> 3,033.91 ± 1.36 ms`)** at $M_g=8$, and speeds up the 288-call contraction **`29.54x` (`18,317.09 ± 20.32 -> 620.01 ± 0.22 ms`)**, the learning rule **`9.91x` (`19,689.83 ± 20.99 -> 1,987.43 ± 0.59 ms`)**, and `fwd + rule` step time **`6.33x` (`21,022.47 ± 21.62 -> 3,319.91 ± 0.59 ms`)** at $M_g=1$.
2. **Complete $2 \times 2$ `{M_g = 8, M_g = 1} x {lr_embed = 3e-3, lr_embed = 0.015}` Factorial (`exp43`) and `scale_factor = 8.0` & $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ Controls (`exp44`):**
   - **Isolating $M_g: 8 \to 1$ Cross-Token Interference vs. $1/M_g$ Normalization on Native `50.3M` (`exp43` + `exp44`):** At `lr_embed = 3e-3`, reducing $M_g: 8 \to 1$ ($G_{\text{micro}}: 1,024 \to 8,192$) under `scale_factor = 1.0` improves 100-step Native `50.3M` Strict Post-Install DEV BPB by **`-0.0256` BPB (`1.9358 ± 0.0002 -> 1.9102 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB, `1.8966 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `215.84` PPL)** in `3,271.7 ms/step` (`fwd + rule`). In `exp44` (`native50m_strict_post_install_M8_scaleMg8_triton`), setting `scale_factor = M_g = 8.0` at $M_g = 8$ (so the $1/M_g$ divisor in `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast` is removed, matching $M_g=1$'s unit prefactor `mult = 1.0`) yields **`1.9350 / 1.9347 / 1.9353` $\to$ `1.9350 ± 0.0003` DEV BPB (`1.9197 ± 0.0003` VAL-B, `231.42` PPL)**—differing from `scale_factor = 1.0` (`1.9358 ± 0.0002`) by only **`-0.0008` BPB** because `Pre-Polar Muon` and `AdamW` are nearly scale-invariant; as a descriptive decomposition along this tested intervention path ($M_g = 8, \text{scale\_factor} = 1.0 \to M_g = 8, \text{scale\_factor} = 8.0 \to M_g = 1, \text{scale\_factor} = 1.0$), eliminating the $M_g - 1 = 7$ within-stream cross-token interference terms accounts for **`96.9%` (`-0.0248` of `-0.0256` BPB, `1.9350 -> 1.9102`)** of the net $M_g: 8 \to 1$ improvement.
   - **Scale-Matched Embedding Velocity (`lr_embed = 0.015`) vs. Stage-3 $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ Damping at $BT = 131,072$ (`exp43` + `exp44`):** Increasing `lr_embed` to `0.015` ($= 0.12 / \sqrt{64}$) under `exp32`'s exact `dfwd_x_final_wte` lexical contraction and $\epsilon = 10^{-8}$ improves $M_g = 8$ by **`-0.0198` BPB (`1.9358 -> 1.9160 ± 0.0003`)** in `2,988.6 ms/step` and improves $M_g = 1$ to **`1.8978 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.8844 ± 0.0003` VAL-B BPB, `208.39` PPL)** in `3,263.7 ms/step` (`-0.0383` BPB ahead of `exp32`). Conversely, in `exp44` (`native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_eps1e4_triton`), changing *only* Stage-3 `lm_head` AdamW `eps` from $10^{-8} \to 10^{-4}$ at $M_g = 1, \text{lr\_embed} = 0.015$ (while preserving `dfwd_x_final_wte` and `lrm: 1.0 -> 0.5`) degrades 100-step Native `50.3M` DEV BPB by **`+0.0288` BPB (`1.8978 ± 0.0007 -> 1.9266 ± 0.0004` DEV BPB, `1.9117 ± 0.0001` VAL-B, `226.01` PPL)**. This empirical contrast is consistent with the batch-size scaling mechanism of Proposition 4(2) and Section 4.3 (`exp36` Part 0A): on `d6` ($BT = 2,048$, `360` steps), single-step target-token collisions are frequent and $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ damps post-installation drift (`-0.0279` BPB), whereas on Native `50.3M` ($BT = 131,072 = 64\times$ larger batch, `100` steps), $1/BT$ already dilutes single-token self-update collisions $64\times$ and $\epsilon = 10^{-4}$ over-damps rare-token `lm_head` adaptation over a 100-step horizon.
3. **Scale-Transfer of 5-Matrix Spherical Active-$W_Q$ (`exp46`), Selective Upper-Half `(4, 5, 6, 7)` Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ at $M=2$ and $M=4$ under $\gamma_K=0.85, \eta_K \in \{0.012, 0.024\}$ (`exp55`, `exp65` Arms 1–2), Closed-Form `Wiener-MMSE` + `Hadamard-4` (`exp61`), Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel `PolarExpress` (`exp65` Arms 3–4, `exp69`), Un-Equilibrated `Exact-SVD Polar` & `Exact-SVD Taper` (`exp75`), Equilibrated `PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `exp74`), Un-Equilibrated `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `exp76`), and Full 8-Block 6-Matrix (`99.9990%` Active Parameters) Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ at $M=1$ and $M=2$ (`exp48`) to Long-Context Native `50,332,176`:**

     1. **Single-Probe Full 8-Block 6-Matrix ($M=1$, `16` Perturbed SDPA Calls/MB, `native50m_strict_post_install_M1_wte15e3_6mat_decoupled_qk_future_g085_triton`):** Achieves **`1.8883 / 1.8877 / 1.8891` $\to$ `1.8884 ± 0.0007` DEV BPB (`1.8753 ± 0.0006` VAL-B BPB, `202.96` PPL)** across Seeds `46, 47, 48` in a 3-seed mean of **`5,116.3 ms/step` (`2,081.1 ms` fwd + `3,035.3 ms` rule)**, **`10.08 GiB` peak VRAM**, and `44 GiB` pinned host RAM—improving upon 5-matrix `exp46` (`1.8897 ± 0.0008`, `6,012.9 ms/step`) by **`-0.0013` BPB (`-0.76` PPL)**.

Furthermore, because Native `50.3M` has double the per-head dimension ($d_h = 128$ vs. $d_h = 64$ on `d6`), $M \in \{2, 4\}$ query/key probes span a smaller fraction $M/d_h \in \{1/64, 1/32\}$ of each head's $128$-dimensional channel space. Moreover, equilibrated **`PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `exp74`)** achieves **`1.8590 / 1.8594 / 1.8592` $\to$ `1.8592 ± 0.0002` DEV BPB (`1.8450 ± 0.0003` VAL-B, `186.99` PPL)** in `5,573.1 ms/step` at $M=2$ (`-0.0158` DEV BPB over unblended `1.8750`, beating unblended $M=4$ `1.8702` by **`-0.0110` DEV BPB at half the SDPA probes and in `759.5 ms/step` less time**) and **`1.8560 / 1.8545 / 1.8556` $\to$ `1.8554 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB (`1.8410 ± 0.0008` VAL-B, `184.97` PPL)** in `6,320.0 ms/step` at $M=4$ (`-0.0148` DEV BPB over `1.8702`—**widening the fixed-token gain over 4-matrix `exp43` (`1.8978 ± 0.0007`) by $3.39\times$ to `-0.0424` DEV BPB (`-0.0434` VAL-B, `-23.42` PPL)** and outperforming CPU-offloaded `exp32` (`1.9361 ± 0.0004` in `7,857.1 ms/step`) by **`-0.0807` DEV BPB (`-47.17` PPL) in `1.24x` less step time**), while un-equilibrated **`Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `exp76` Arms 21–22)** reaches **`1.8616 / 1.8558 / 1.8576` $\to$ `1.8583 ± 0.0030` DEV BPB (`1.8442 ± 0.0029` VAL-B, `186.53` PPL)** in `6,036.0 ms/step` at $M=2$ (`-0.0167` DEV BPB over `1.8750` and `-0.0009` BPB ahead of linear `PrePolar-Blend` `1.8592`) and **`1.8649 / 1.8554 / 1.8583` $\to$ `1.8595 ± 0.0049` DEV BPB (`1.8452 ± 0.0046` VAL-B, `187.16` PPL)** in `6,886.6 ms/step` at $M=4$.

---

### 4.9 Superposed 4-Stage Post-Install Dual 1-Hop (MLP + Attention-Value) Highway WM (`exp77`, Proposition 10)

Having driven 0-hop 2-stage post-install Delayed WM to `2.0337 ± 0.0005` DEV BPB (`360` steps) and `1.9407 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB (`720` steps) via un-equilibrated cubic Gram spectral tapering (`Gram3-PrePolar-Blend`, `exp76`), we test **Proposition 10** (`exp77_dual_1hop_highway_wm.py` and `exp77_dual_1hop_highway_wm_results.json`, `57` 3-seed arms = `171` runs across Seeds `46, 47, 48` on ClimbMix `d6` [$D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$] and 12-layer `d12` [$L=12, D=384$]). Throughout `exp77`–`exp79`, `reverse_error_matmuls_per_step = 0` denotes **zero reverse error matrix multiplications through Transformer backbone layer weights ($W_{\text{fc}}, W_{\text{proj}}, W_{q,k,v}$)**; we explicitly separate **Class I (`1hop_mlp_superposed4`, zero reverse matrix multiplications through backbone weights and zero attention-probability transpose gathers $A_i^\top u_v$)** from **Class II (`1hop_mlp_val_superposed4`, zero reverse matrix multiplications through backbone weights, plus a local causal attention-probability transpose gather $\tilde{u}_{v, n}^{(i, h)} = \sum_{\tau \ge n} A_{\tau, n}^{(i, h)} u_{v, \tau}^{(i, h)}$ computed from cached forward attention weights $A^{(i, h)}$)**:

**Table 8:** Controlled 3-seed evaluation of **Proposition 10 (`exp77_dual_1hop_highway_wm_results.json`)** across Surrogate-BP controls (`0-hop`, `1-hop MLP`, `all-hop MLP`, `1-hop Value`, and `Dual 1-Hop MLP+Value`), non-intervening-install 4-stage controls (`delayed_4stage`), Class I Superposed 4-Stage 1-Hop MLP Highway WM (`zero` reverse error matmuls through backbone weights and `zero` local attention-probability transpose gathers), Class II Superposed 4-Stage Dual 1-Hop (MLP + Attention-Value) Highway WM, width scaling ($D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$), and 12-layer depth scaling (`d12`, $L=12, D=384$).

**Key Findings of `exp77` (Proposition 10):**
1. **Why 0-Hop Sub-Block Direct-Skip Plateaus and How Dual 1-Hop (MLP + Value) Surrogate BP Restores Depth Composition:** Under exact `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$) on `d6`, adding 1-hop MLP highways to 0-hop Sub-Block-Skip BP improves 720-step DEV BPB by **`-0.0172` BPB (`1.8941 ± 0.0013 -> 1.8769 ± 0.0011`)**—capturing `94.5%` of the gain of full all-hop MLP highways (`1.8760 ± 0.0012`)—while adding 1-hop Attention-Value highways alone yields `1.8869 ± 0.0006` (`-0.0072` BPB), and combining **Dual 1-Hop (MLP + Value) Highways (`1hop_mlp_plus_val_highway`) reaches `1.9983 ± 0.0002` at 360 steps and `1.8731 ± 0.0003` (`1.8380` VAL-B, `171.27` PPL) at 720 steps** (`-0.0210` BPB ahead of 0-hop `1.8941`, recovering `54.9%` of the gap to Full Global BP `1.8559 ± 0.0006` under matched `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` and `51.1%` vs. unblended Global BP `1.8530`). On 12-layer `d12` ($L=12, D=384$), Dual 1-Hop Surrogate BP improves over 0-hop Sub-Block-Skip BP by **`-0.0236` BPB (`1.8840 ± 0.0004 -> 1.8604 ± 0.0011` at 720 steps, vs. `1.8378 ± 0.0008` Global BP)**.
2. **Deconfounding `delayed_4stage` vs. `superposed_4stage` and Class I (`1-Hop MLP`) vs. Class II (`Dual 1-Hop MLP+Val`):** Simply delaying 0-hop WM to a 4-stage buffer without intervening installation (`wm_0hop_delayed4_beta000`: `2.0408` at 360st, `1.9435 ± 0.0020` at 720st) slightly *degrades* accuracy relative to 2-stage 0-hop WM (`2.0337` at 360st, `1.9407` at 720st) due to 2 extra steps of pipeline staleness. By contrast, **Superposed 4-Stage Post-Install Construction (`superposed_4stage`)** installs 0-hop Stage-1 weights at $t-2$ while accumulating post-install 1-hop highway Stage-1 weights at $t-4$:
   - **Class I (1-Hop MLP Highway Only, Zero $A_i^\top u_v$ Gathers):** Achieves **`2.0334 ± 0.0001` (`35.49s`; `2.0332 ± 0.0003` in `19.56s` with pre-combined `qk_p4comb`) at 360 steps** and **`1.9362 ± 0.0003` (`54.85s`; `1.9357 ± 0.0002` in `113.94s` under `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper`; `1.9355 ± 0.0006` in `52.58s` with `qk_p4comb`) at 720 steps**—improving over 2-stage 0-hop WM (`1.9407 ± 0.0006`) by **`-0.0052` DEV BPB (`-2.88` PPL)** and beating 720-step 4-matrix Exact Backpropagation (`1.9423 ± 0.0031`) by **`-0.0068` DEV BPB** with zero reverse matmuls and zero attention pullbacks.
   - **Class II (Dual 1-Hop MLP + Attention-Value Highway):** Adding the 1-hop Attention-Value highway (`wm_1hop_mlp_val_superposed4`) leaps to **`2.0293 ± 0.0005` (`2.0291 ± 0.0005`, `1.9914` VAL-B, `262.90` PPL in `24.12s` with `qk_p4comb`) at 360 steps**, **`1.9247 ± 0.0008` (`47.02s` at $\beta_{\text{mlp}}=\beta_{\text{val}}=0.75$) and `1.9238 ± 0.0008` (`1.8888` VAL-B, `196.91` PPL, `73.17s` with `qk_p4comb`) at 720 steps**, and **`1.9014 ± 0.0009` (`1.8664` VAL-B, `185.12` PPL, `47.53s`) at 840 steps** (improving over 840-step 0-hop `1.9217 ± 0.0003` by **`-0.0203` DEV BPB, `-10.63` PPL**!). Across all four widths $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$ at 360 steps, Class II reaches **`2.0606 ± 0.0006, 2.0293 ± 0.0005 [2.0291 qk_p4comb], 2.0157 ± 0.0009, 2.0082 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB** (improving over 0-hop `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` `2.0640, 2.0337, 2.0206, 2.0138` by `-0.0034` to `-0.0056` BPB at every width), and on 12-layer `d12` ($L=12, D=384$, 720 steps) it improves over 0-hop WM (`1.9370 ± 0.0011` in `99.92s`) by **`-0.0131` DEV BPB (`-7.18` PPL)** to **`1.9239 ± 0.0006` (`1.8889` VAL-B, `196.95` PPL, `141.59s`)**—matching 6-layer `d6` (`1.9247`/`1.9238`) without depth degradation.
3. **Fixed-State Simultaneous Contraction Alignment Audit (`audit_exp77_mechanisms.py`, `exp77_dual_1hop_highway_wm_results.json`):** To isolate the geometric alignment of 0-hop, 1-hop Value (`1hop_val_wm`), 1-hop MLP (`1hop_mlp_wm`), and Dual 1-Hop MLP+Value (`dual_1hop_mlp_val_wm`) contractions against Full Global Backpropagation and against each method's corresponding Surrogate-BP target (`audit_checkpoint_cosines` in `audit_exp77_mechanisms.py`), we evaluate a fixed-state single-draw simultaneous diagnostic at $\sigma = 10^{-3}$ ($G = BT$) across the 5 linear/separable matrix families (`wte`, `attn.c_v`, `attn.c_proj`, `mlp.c_fc`, `mlp.c_proj`, excluding $W_Q, W_K$ and without the $\sigma = 0.02$ delayed pipeline's intervening weight installations) on a ClimbMix `d6` model at Step 0 and after 120 steps of Global-BP warmup (`step_0_cosine_alignment` and `step_120_cosine_alignment` in `exp77_dual_1hop_highway_wm_results.json`). At **Step 0**, progression across `0hop_wm` $\to$ `1hop_val_wm` $\to$ `1hop_mlp_wm` $\to$ `dual_1hop_mlp_val_wm` lifts cosine alignment with Full Global BP on `wte` from `0.9143 -> 0.9151 -> 0.9897 -> 0.9905`, and across the 6-layer mean on `attn.c_v` from `0.9610 -> 0.9647 -> 0.9911 -> 0.9969` (`L0: 0.9081 -> 0.9902`), `attn.c_proj` from `0.9591 -> 0.9639 -> 0.9909 -> 0.9968` (`L0: 0.9077 -> 0.9901`), `mlp.c_fc` from `0.9583 -> 0.9588 -> 0.9964 -> 0.9969` (`L0: 0.9103 -> 0.9920`), and `mlp.c_proj` from `0.9709 -> 0.9734 -> 0.9950 -> 0.9982` (`L0: 0.9369 -> 0.9952`). At **Step 120**, the same progression lifts cosine alignment with Full Global BP on `wte` from `0.8322 -> 0.8377 -> 0.9682 -> 0.9731`, and across the 6-layer mean on `attn.c_v` from `0.8969 -> 0.9213 -> 0.9706 -> 0.9878` (`L0: 0.7299 -> 0.9598`), `attn.c_proj` from `0.9061 -> 0.9227 -> 0.9733 -> 0.9866` (`L0: 0.7650 -> 0.9608`), `mlp.c_fc` from `0.9415 -> 0.9446 -> 0.9890 -> 0.9917` (`L0: 0.8418 -> 0.9764`), and `mlp.c_proj` from `0.9408 -> 0.9482 -> 0.9851 -> 0.9913` (`L0: 0.8437 -> 0.9758`), while achieving `0.9978–0.9993` cosine with each method's corresponding Surrogate-BP gradient (`vs_own_surrogate_bp_mean`).

---

### 4.10 3-Stage (`t-2`, Zero Backbone Reverse Matmuls) Low-Rank Predictive (`LP-WM`) and Oja-Stiefel Coordinate-Transport Bridge (`Oja-LP-WM`) Weight Maximization (`exp78` & `exp79`, Proposition 11)

While Class II Superposed 4-Stage Dual 1-Hop Highway WM (`exp77`) achieves `1.9238 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB on `d6` (`720` steps) and `1.9239 ± 0.0006` on `d12` (`141.59s`), it requires a **4-stage (`t-4`) ring buffer**, $O(L^2)$ pairwise highway evaluations, and local causal attention-probability transpose gathers $\tilde{u}_v = A_i^\top u_v$. In `exp78` (`exp78_local_predictive_wm.py` and `exp78_linear_predictive_bridge_wm_results.json`, `9` 3-seed arms = `27` runs across Seeds `42, 43, 44`) and `exp79` (`exp79_oja_stiefel_bridge_and_d12_scaling.py` and `exp79_oja_stiefel_bridge_and_d12_scaling_results.json`, `18` 3-seed arms = `54` runs across Seeds `42, 43, 44`), we evaluate **Proposition 11**: a **strictly 3-stage (`t-2` pipeline depth), $O(L)$ per-step complexity, global-backward-free (`backbone_reverse_error_matmuls_per_step = 0` for $W_{\text{fc}}, W_{\text{proj}}, W_{q,k,v}$ and zero attention-probability transpose gathers $A_i^\top u_v$)** inter-layer credit bridge using block-local rank-$r$ ($r=64$) auxiliary coordinate-transport matrices $(U_l, V_l) \in \mathbb{R}^{D \times r}$ updated strictly by local forward-displacement outer products and evaluated via a **2-call column-isolated local bridge pullback per sub-block** (`compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast(U_l, ...)` followed by `compute_stream_wm_rewards_triton_fast(V_l.t(), ...)`, vs. a 1-call input-space scalar-contracted bridge recipe `oja_lp_scalar` with zero local bridge pullbacks):
