**Table 9:** Controlled 3-seed evaluation of **Proposition 11 (`exp78_linear_predictive_bridge_wm_results.json` and `exp79_oja_stiefel_bridge_and_d12_scaling_results.json`, `27` 3-seed arms = `81` runs across Seeds `42, 43, 44`)** across Surrogate-BP coordinate-transport bridge controls (`LP`, `LP-MH`, `Oja Pure-State`, `Oja Joint-Head`), 1-call scalar-contracted vs. 2-call column-isolated local bridge pullback recipes, and 3-stage (`t-2`, zero reverse error matmuls through Transformer backbone weights, zero $A_i^\top u_v$ attention pullbacks) Linear Predictive (`LP-WM`, `LP-MH-WM`) and Oja-Stiefel Coordinate-Transport Bridge (`Oja-LP-WM`, `Oja-LP-MH-WM`) on 6-layer `d6` ($L=6, D=384$) and 12-layer `d12` ($L=12, D=384$).

**Key Findings of `exp78` & `exp79` (Proposition 11):**
1. **Surrogate-BP Coordinate-Transport Ceiling & Joint-Head vs. Pure-State Deconfounding (`exp78` & `exp79`):** On 6-layer `d6` (`360` steps, Seeds `42, 43, 44`), replacing the identity residual projection $P=I$ (`d6_360st_surrogate_subblock_skip_P_I`: `2.0065 ± 0.0014` DEV BPB, `247.06` PPL) with the rank-64 linear predictive coordinate-transport bridge $P_l = I + \beta_{\text{pred}} U_l V_l^\top$ (`d6_360st_surrogate_lowrank_bridge_r64`: `2.0001 ± 0.0011` DEV BPB, `1.9628` VAL-B, `242.78` PPL) improves DEV BPB by **`-0.0063 ± 0.0004` (`-4.28` PPL, $p = 0.0011$)** and lifts Layer-0 gradient cosine vs. Full Global BP from `0.625 -> 0.727`. Furthermore, deconfounding Oja-Stiefel pure state-alignment (`d6_360st_surrogate_oja_bridge_r64_pure_state`: `2.0066 ± 0.0010`) against joint-head alignment (`d6_360st_surrogate_oja_bridge_r64_joint_head`: `2.0049 ± 0.0013` on `d6`, $-0.0018 \pm 0.0003$, $p = 0.0103$; and `2.0031 ± 0.0007 -> 2.0011 ± 0.0012` on 12-layer `d12`, $-0.0021 \pm 0.0007$, $p = 0.0369$, lifting Layer-0 gradient cosine vs. Global BP `1.9890 ± 0.0007` from `0.562 -> 0.629`) confirms that joint-head residual transport is required for inter-layer credit gain.
2. **2-Call Column-Isolated Local Bridge Pullback Recipe vs. 1-Call Input-Space Scalar-Contracted Bridge Recipe (`exp79`):** In `d6_360st_wm_3stage_oja_lp_scalar_r64` (`2.0371 ± 0.0004`) and `d6_360st_wm_3stage_oja_lp_scalar_mh_r64` (`2.0375 ± 0.0006`), `is_scalar_oja` (`exp78_local_predictive_wm.py`) contracts $V_l U_l^\top e_l$ via a single scalar-contracted outgoing-weight WM call using input-space Rademacher signs $\xi_h \sim \operatorname{Unif}(\{\pm 1\}^D)$ without materializing $u_z = U_l^\top e_l$, which incurs Proposition 11(b)'s $(D - 1)\|V_l U_l^\top e_l\|_2^2 \approx (D - 1)\|U_l^\top e_l\|_2^2$ input-space Rademacher variance (`383x` signal energy at $D=384$ under exact orthogonality, or $(r-1)\|U_l^\top e_l\|_2^2 = 63\times$ if signs are drawn in $\mathbb{R}^r$) and also sets `scalar_mode=True` (skipping Step-1 cross-covariance seeding, setting pre-schedule bridge learning rates $(\eta_V, \eta_U) = (0.006, 0.006)$ instead of $(0.04, 0.004)$, and normalizing the columns of $U_l$ in the encoder update), degrading 360-step WM by `+0.0038` to `+0.0042` BPB relative to 0-hop (`2.0333 ± 0.0009`). Our **2-call column-isolated local bridge pullback recipe** (`d6_360st_wm_3stage_oja_lp_r64`: `2.0335 ± 0.0006`; `mh_r64`: `2.0335 ± 0.0004`, which combines Step-1 cross-covariance seeding, $(\eta_V, \eta_U) = (0.04, 0.004)$, unnormalized $U_l$ encoder updates, and 2 column-isolated Triton outgoing-weight WM kernel calls per sub-block to evaluate the exact two-map local bridge pullback $V_l(U_l^\top e_l)$ across $(U_l, V_l)$ at $M_g=1$ with zero conditional variance and zero reverse matmuls through Transformer backbone weights) improves over the scalar-contracted bridge recipe by **`-0.0036` to `-0.0041` DEV BPB ($p \le 0.0016$)** across all 3 seeds (as noted in Proposition 11(b), because `scalar_mode=True` differs in those auxiliary bridge hyperparameters as well as the Stage-2 contraction, this empirical contrast compares the two complete bridge recipes rather than isolating the Stage-2 contraction alone).
3. **720-Step `d6` and 12-Layer Depth Scaling (`d12`: $L=12, D=384$) & Quality–Time Tradeoff vs. 4-Stage Dual 1-Hop WM:** On `d6` (`720` steps), all four 3-stage coordinate-bridge WM arms—`d6_720st_wm_3stage_lp_lowrank_r64` (`1.9373 ± 0.0003` in `30.86s`), `d6_720st_wm_3stage_lp_multi_horizon_r64` (`1.9370 ± 0.0004`, `1.9017` VAL-B, `204.13` PPL in `34.44s`), `d6_720st_wm_3stage_oja_lp_r64` (`1.9371 ± 0.0006`, `1.9017` VAL-B, `204.17` PPL in `32.78s`), and `d6_720st_wm_3stage_oja_lp_mh_r64` (`1.9371 ± 0.0007` in `36.13s`)—improve over the matched-trainer 3-stage 0-hop baseline (`d6_720st_wm_3stage_0hop_baseline`: `1.9410 ± 0.0006` in `27.82s`) by **`-0.0037` to `-0.0040` DEV BPB (`-2.06` to `-2.27` PPL)** at only `+10.9%` to `+17.8%` step time, approaching 4-stage Class I 1-Hop MLP WM (`1.9362 ± 0.0003` in `54.85s`) in **`1.67x`–`1.78x` less training time** while keeping a 3-stage (`t-2`) buffer and zero $A_i^\top u_v$ gathers. Crucially, when depth doubles from 6 layers (`d6`) to 12 layers (`d12`, $L=12$), the 720-step gain of 3-stage `Oja-LP-WM` / `Oja-LP-MH-WM` over 0-hop WM grows from **`-0.0039` BPB on `d6` (`1.9410 -> 1.9371`) to `-0.0047` / `-0.0049` BPB on `d12` (`1.9384 ± 0.0015 -> 1.9337 ± 0.0007` in `61.58s` / `1.9335 ± 0.0006`, `1.8989` VAL-B, `202.21` PPL in `68.11s`)**—**statistically significantly outperforming 6-layer `d6` 720-step `Oja-LP-WM` (`1.9371 ± 0.0006`) by `-0.0036 ± 0.0009` DEV BPB (95% CI `[-0.0058, -0.0013]`, $t = -6.82, p = 0.0209$)** and providing a **`2.08x`–`2.30x` faster 3-stage (`t-2`, zero $A_i^\top u_v$ gathers) quality–time tradeoff relative to 4-stage `d12` Dual 1-Hop WM (`1.9239 ± 0.0006` in `141.59s`, which achieves `-0.0096` BPB better accuracy at $2.08\times$ higher runtime)**.

---

## 5. Limitations and Scope

1. **Short Developmental Horizon, Microbatch Accumulation, Measured Training-Region Timing, Fixed-Token vs. Matched-Time Trade-offs, and Gap to Full Backpropagation:** Our experiments use $0.737\text{M}$–$2.056\text{M}$ tokens on `d6` ($360$–$1,004$ steps across $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$) and 12-layer `d12` ($360$–$720$ steps at $L=12, D=384$), and $13.1\text{M}$ tokens on Native `50.3M` ($100$ steps), $4 \times 16$ microbatch accumulation on Native `50.3M`, and repeatedly inspected `DEV`/`VAL-B` validation splits (neither validation split provides an untouched confirmation of the selected upper-half, `wiener_mmse`, per-head Stiefel, linear/cubic spectral-tapering, superposed 1-hop dual-highway, or Oja-Stiefel coordinate-bridge recipes, and the upper-half efficiency advantage is specific to this trainer, probe allocation, and short horizon). All `Run GPU` times in Tables 1–9 and Figure 1E measure CUDA event/synchronized intervals across the forward pass and learning-rule update (`total_train_gpu_s_mean`), excluding data loading, host-to-device batch transfer, and validation passes; moreover, on `d6`, the specialized 4-matrix `exp37`/`exp39` trainer runs at `~18.8 ms/step` (`6.73s` at 360 steps, `13.52s` at 720 steps), whereas the general 6-matrix-capable `exp49`–`exp79` trainer runs at `~21.6 ms/step` for 4-matrix (`15.62s` at 720 steps, `21.55s` at 1,004 steps). On Native `50.3M`, the GPU-resident 4-matrix `exp43` trainer trades `9.11 -> 20.57–20.65 GiB` peak VRAM to eliminate host-device trace transfers (`2.99–3.27 s/step` `fwd + rule`), whereas full pinned host ring buffers (`exp44`, `exp46`, `exp48`, `exp55`, `exp61`, `exp65`, `exp69`, `exp74`–`exp76`) retain **`9.61–10.36 GiB` peak GPU VRAM** while allocating up to **`44–50 GiB` of pinned host RAM** and incurring higher per-step latency (`4.87–6.89 s/step` 3-seed mean `fwd + rule`); consequently, the improvements of 5-matrix `exp46` (`1.8897 ± 0.0008`), Upper-Half `(4..7)` `exp55` (`1.8881 ± 0.0010` at $M=2$ in `5.68 s/step`; `1.8858 ± 0.0011` at $M=4$ in `6.57 s/step`, which is a fixed-token/probe-budget comparison whose step time exceeds Full 8-Block $M=2$'s `5.81 s/step`), 6-matrix `exp48` (`1.8884 ± 0.0007` at $M=1$, `1.8853 ± 0.0013` at $M=2$), Upper-Half `(4..7)` `Wiener-MMSE` `exp61` (`1.8849 ± 0.0003` at $M=2$ in `5.56 s/step`, which beats Full 8-Block $M=2$ $\gamma_K=0.85$ in both accuracy and step time at half the SDPA probes; and `1.8803 ± 0.0007` [`1.8668` VAL-B, `198.42` PPL] at $M=4$ `Wiener-MMSE + Hadamard-4` in `6.29 s/step`), Upper-Half `(4..7)` Per-Head Stiefel `exp65`/`exp69` (`1.8779/1.8750` at $M=2$ in `5.60 s/step` and `1.8729/1.8702` at $M=4$ in `6.33 s/step`), Un-Equilibrated `Exact-SVD Polar` & `Exact-SVD Taper` `exp75` (`1.8747 ± 0.0005` and `1.8620 ± 0.0027` at $M=2$ in `6.24 s/step`), Equilibrated `PrePolar-Blend` `exp74` (`1.8592 ± 0.0002` at $M=2$ in `5.57 s/step` and **`1.8554 ± 0.0008` [`1.8410` VAL-B, `184.97` PPL] at $M=4$ in `6.32 s/step`**), and Un-Equilibrated `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` `exp76` (**`1.8583 ± 0.0030` [`1.8442` VAL-B, `186.53` PPL] at $M=2$ in `6.04 s/step`** and `1.8595 ± 0.0049` at $M=4$ in `6.89 s/step`) over 4-matrix `exp43` (`1.8978 ± 0.0007`) are fixed-token (`100`-step) accuracy and GPU VRAM-footprint gains rather than equal-wall-clock compute advantages. On `d6`, inside the same `exp49`–`exp79` trainer, uniform full-depth 6-matrix Decoupled Active-$(W_Q, W_K)$ $M=2$ is **unfavorable at equal training-region time** against extra-step 4-matrix WM because lower-block `(0, 1, 2)` SDPA probes add $+16.6\%$ step time for only $-0.0013$ to $-0.0023$ BPB conditional gain: at the `~9.35s` budget, 438-step 4-matrix (`2.0454 ± 0.0008` in `9.39s`) beats 360-step Full 6-matrix $M=2$ (`2.0595 ± 0.0007` in `9.35s`), and at the `~21.6s` budget, 1,004-step 4-matrix (`1.9720 ± 0.0006` in `21.55s`) beats 720-step Full 6-matrix $M=2$ (`1.9763 ± 0.0009` in `21.67s` / `1.9756 ± 0.0017` in `21.77s`). However, restricting $(W_Q, W_K)$ probes to the **Upper-Half blocks `(3, 4, 5)`** (`exp50`–`exp76`, where `exp63` Arms 4–7 provide a recipe-specific descriptive contrast at 720 steps in which the Global-BP `Upper-Half vs. Lower-Half` gap [`-0.0031` BPB, `1.8759 vs. 1.8790`] is `20.0%` [`-0.0031 / -0.0155`] of the Sub-Block-Skip gap [`-0.0155` BPB, `1.9075 vs. 1.9230`], while the remaining `80.0%` [`-0.0124 / -0.0155`] arises when inter-block and intra-block `attn -> mlp` paths are detached) captures `90.1%`–`94.4%` of the 720-step full-depth gain (`1.9779 ± 0.0006` in `18.59s` main-RNG-matched; `1.9769 ± 0.0021` in `16.09s` layer-RNG-synced), captures `85.6%`–`92.3%` of the 360-step Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ gain across all four widths $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$ at half the SDPA probes, matches 438-step 4-matrix at 400 steps (`2.0454 ± 0.0012` DEV, `2.0085 ± 0.0015` VAL-B vs. `2.0092 ± 0.0006`), **beats both uniform 720-step Full 6-matrix $M=2$ (`1.9756`/`1.9763`) and 1,004-step 4-matrix (`1.9720` in `21.55s`) when all 24 SDPA probes/step are concentrated on Upper-Half `(3, 4, 5)` $M=4$ at 720 steps (`1.9699 ± 0.0001` in `21.05s` [`g085`], and `1.9672 ± 0.0017` in `22.36s` [`wiener_mmse`]) or 740 steps (`1.9670 ± 0.0002` in `19.19s`), and beats 1,004-step 4-matrix by `-0.0111` to `-0.0383` DEV BPB (`-6.70` to `-22.25` PPL) at `~19.7–22.9s` training-region time (`840` steps of Upper-Half $M=2$: `1.9609 ± 0.0006` in `21.71s` [`g085`], `1.9594 ± 0.0019` in `20.21s` [`wiener_mmse + topk32`], `1.9524 ± 0.0004` in `19.91s` [`Per-Head Stiefel`, `exp64` Arm 6], `1.9509 ± 0.0003` in `19.67s` [`Fixed-QK+V`, `exp70` Arm 4], `1.9420 ± 0.0004` [`1.9073` VAL-B, `206.99` PPL] in `20.03s` [`PrePolar-Blend` $\gamma_{QK}=0.20$, `exp73` Arm 3], and `1.9337 ± 0.0015` [`1.8990` VAL-B, `202.29` PPL] in `22.90s` [`Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` $\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$, `exp76` Arm 13])**. While 360-step Full 6-Matrix $M=4$ under Per-Head Rectangular Stiefel `PolarExpress`, linear/cubic spectral tapering, 3-Stage Oja-Stiefel Coordinate Bridge, and Superposed 1-Hop Dual Highway (`2.0675/2.0640/2.0606, 2.0382/2.0377/2.0337/2.0335/2.0293 [2.0291 qk_p4comb], 2.0259/2.0206/2.0157, 2.0207/2.0138/2.0082` across $D \in \{256, 384, 512, 768\}$) strictly beats both the smaller 4-matrix Exact Backpropagation reference (`2.0789, 2.0532, 2.0442, 2.0433`, which leaves $W_Q, W_K$ frozen) by `-0.0183` to `-0.0351` DEV BPB **and Shared-LR Full 6-Matrix Exact Backpropagation (`2.0590, 2.0316, 2.0191, 2.0153`) across widths $D \in \{384, 512, 768\}$ by `-0.0025` to `-0.0071` DEV BPB**—and at 720 steps ($D=384$), `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper` (`1.9408 ± 0.0007`), `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` (`1.9407 ± 0.0006` in `29.07s`), `Gavish-Donoho` [13] Optimal Shrinkage (`1.9401 ± 0.0021`), 3-Stage `LP-MH-WM` / `Oja-LP-WM` (`1.9370 ± 0.0004` in `34.44s` / `1.9371 ± 0.0006` in `32.78s`), 4-Stage Class I `1-Hop MLP Highway` (`1.9362` / `1.9355 ± 0.0006` in `52.58s`), and 4-Stage Class II `Superposed 1-Hop Dual Highway` (`1.9247 ± 0.0008` in `47.02s`, and `1.9238 ± 0.0008` in `73.17s`) strictly beat 720-step 4-matrix Exact Backpropagation (`1.9423 ± 0.0031`) by up to **`-0.0185` DEV BPB (`-9.41` PPL)**—all backward-free arms still trail `Per-Head QK/QKV` Full 6-Matrix `Muon+AdamW` Backpropagation (`1.9934` / `1.9916` / `1.9899` / `1.9879` at 360 steps and `1.8549` / `1.8536` / `1.8530` / `1.8518` at 720 steps on `d6`; `1.9800` at 360st and `1.8378` at 720st on 12-layer `d12`; `1.6989 ± 0.0005` on 100-step Native `50.3M`), and the `d6` diagonal-skip + `lrm -> 0.2, eps = 1e-4` package (`exp40` `1.9845` and `exp41` `2.0252`) does not transfer directly to 100-step Native `50.3M`. Furthermore, our perturbation comparators (`SR-LNP` and `SR-NP`) evaluate author-defined recipes on the 4-matrix subset rather than QLabs' closed-source Dust training pipeline [9].
2. **Perturbation-Differential Plasticity, $\operatorname{diag}(1-x_i^2/D)$ Coordinate Scaling, Stage-2 Leverage Inversion, and 0-Hop vs. 0+1-Hop Surrogate Comparisons (`exp36`, `exp57`, `exp58`, `exp60`, `exp63`, `exp77`):** Our reward contraction evaluates a stream-partitioned perturbation-differential plasticity tensor $\Delta \tilde{W}^{(m+1, g)}$ against live receiver weights $W^{(m+1)}(t_{\text{constr}})$, and Proposition 3 uses the diagonal skip $W_{\text{skip}} = \alpha_0 I_D$ as an algebraic surrogate. As shown in Proposition 1(c), Proposition 10(a), `exp58`, `exp60`, and `exp77`, coordinatewise WM followed by radial projection (`project_out_forward_radial_nullspace`) has first-order conditional mean $\mathbb{E}[P_x\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}] = \frac{1}{\text{rms}(z)}P_x\operatorname{diag}(1 - x_i^2/D)a \neq \frac{1}{\text{rms}(z)}P_x a = \mu_{\text{skip}}$ (with modulation-vector discrepancy bounded by $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a\|_2}{\text{rms}(z)}$ and outer-product matrix Frobenius discrepancy bounded by $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a\|_2 \|h_n\|_2}{\text{rms}(z)}$, where the normalized discrepancy coefficient $\max_i x_i^2/D$ is $O(1/D)$ only under coordinate concentration $\max_i x_i^2 = O(1)$; while inserting the closed-form leverage inverse $\operatorname{diag}(1-x_i^2/D)^{-1}$ prior to $P_x$ restores exact equality $\cos = 1.000000$ at $D=3$ and $D=4$ [`< 1e-14` max error on 256 patterns using the full first-order tangent displacement $(P_x\xi)_d$ in `verify_paper5.py` Check 4G] and is evaluated on 0-hop WM in `exp60` Arms 3 & 6 [`2.0519` at 360st, `1.9632` at 720st], the `exp77` training runs in `exp77_1hop_mlp_wm_engine.py` apply direct radial projection $P_x$ without $L(x)$), and as shown in `exp57`–`exp58`, `exp63`, and `exp77` (cf. [5]), 0-hop Sub-Block Direct-Skip Surrogate BP (`1.9819` on 4-Mat; `1.9367` shared-LR / `1.9016` exact `exp49` 6-Mat opt / `1.8920` `Fixed-QK+V` / `1.8941` `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` at 720 steps) omits inter-block composition and intra-block `attn -> mlp` credit, whereas **0+1-Hop (MLP + Attention-Value) Surrogate BP (`exp77`: `1.9983 ± 0.0002` at 360st and `1.8731 ± 0.0003` at 720st on `d6`; `1.9912 ± 0.0008` at 360st and `1.8604 ± 0.0011` at 720st on 12-layer `d12`) restores `62.0%` (360st) and `54.9%` (720st) of the gap to matched `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` Full Global BP (`1.9929` / `1.8559` on `d6`; `51.1%` on `d12` `1.8840 -> 1.8604` vs. `1.8378`) while remaining in fixed $O(1)$ 4-stage depth** (though still omitting $\ge 2$-hop compositions and 1-hop query-key attention-map derivatives). Moreover, as shown in `exp36` Part B, folding internal `Pre-Polar Muon` (`2.1613` vs. `2.1579`) or `lm_head` `AdamW` preconditioning (`2.2128` vs. `2.1579`) into the reader degrades learning because forward perturbations propagate through $W_{\text{live}}$.

While Proposition 7 (Zhang et al. [12]), Proposition 8, Proposition 9 (together with Gavish & Donoho [13]), Proposition 10, and Proposition 11 establish the theoretical properties of un-equilibrated groupwise/headwise polar factors, un-equilibrated `Exact-SVD Taper`, un-equilibrated `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper` / `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend`, `Superposed 4-Stage Post-Install Dual 1-Hop Highway WM`, and `3-Stage Low-Rank Predictive / Oja-Stiefel Coordinate-Transport Bridge WM`—namely Frobenius norm preservation $\|U\|_F^2 = m$, elimination of cross-head Gram rotation, the nuclear-norm inequality $\sum_h \|G_h\|_* \ge \|G\|_*$, exact singular-vector preservation with linear ($s_r(\gamma)$) and cubic ($s_r^{(3)}(\gamma)$) singular-value tapering, escaping the 2-pass equilibration diagonal collapse on $\operatorname{diag}(4,2,1,1)$ via $G_3 = (G_n G_n^\top) G_n$, the conditional expectation of 1-hop MLP + Attention-Value highway estimators in a fixed 4-slot post-install ring buffer (alongside the theoretical leverage-inversion corollary), and zero conditional variance of 2-call column-isolated local bridge pullbacks vs. $(D-1)\|V_l U_l^\top e_l\|_2^2$ input-space (and $(r-1)\|U_l^\top e_l\|_2^2$ latent-space) variance of 1-call scalar-contracted bridge transport—we explicitly qualify all theoretical and causal claims in the backward-free context: (a) in Delayed WM, $\sum_h \|\hat{G}_h\|_* \ge \|\hat{G}\|_*$ measures self-alignment of $U(\hat{G})$ with the noisy perturbation estimate $\hat{G} = \bar{G} + \Delta$ rather than guaranteed alignment with the population gradient $\bar{G}$, and small observed singular values $\hat{\sigma}_r$ in empirical checkpoints do not by themselves prove that a singular mode is pure perturbation noise; (b) Proposition 8 governs un-equilibrated `Exact-SVD Taper` (`exact_svd_taper_batched` in `run_exp73`, `run_exp65`) rather than equilibrated `PrePolar-Blend` (`prepolar_blend_batched`, which applies 2-pass row/column $\ell_2$ equilibration before blending and maps any positive diagonal matrix such as $\operatorname{diag}(4,2,1,1) \mapsto I_4$), whereas in Proposition 9, `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` (`gram3_prepolar_blend_batched` in `run_exp76`, `run_exp65`) forms its cubic skip branch $G_3 = (G_n G_n^\top) G_n$ directly from the **un-equilibrated** tensor $G_n = \hat{G}_h / \|\hat{G}_h\|_F$, matching `Exact-SVD Gram3 Taper` on $\operatorname{diag}(4,2,1,1)$ to $< 10^{-15}$ and within `0.0000–0.0001` DEV BPB on `d6` (`2.0337` vs. `2.0337` at 360st; `1.9407` vs. `1.9408` at 720st); (c) while linear `PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_{QK}=0.20$) slightly improves 360/720-step Global BP (`1.9899 -> 1.9879 ± 0.0006`; `1.8530 -> 1.8518 ± 0.0006`) and Sub-Block-Skip BP (`2.0052 -> 2.0039 ± 0.0007`; `1.8920 -> 1.8904 ± 0.0009`) whereas cubic `Gram3-PrePolar-Blend` ($\gamma_Q=0.20, \gamma_K=0.35$) **degrades** noise-free Global BP (`1.9899 -> 1.9929 ± 0.0002` at 360st; `1.8530 -> 1.8559 ± 0.0006` at 720st, `exp76` Arms 17–18) and Sub-Block-Skip BP (`2.0052 -> 2.0071 ± 0.0012` at 360st; `1.8920 -> 1.8941 ± 0.0013` at 720st, `exp76` Arms 19–20) while improving noisy Delayed WM by **`-0.0106` BPB (`2.0443 -> 2.0337`) at 360st and `-0.0141` BPB (`1.9548 -> 1.9407`) at 720st**, we report this double dissociation as a controlled empirical contrast across credit engines rather than a formal proof of trajectory-independent noise attribution because BP and WM trajectories also differ in credit delay, filtered key/value targets, and accumulated optimizer states; (d) in Proposition 10 (`exp77`), deconfounding `superposed_4stage` (`2.0291` at 360st, `1.9247`/`1.9238` at 720st) against the non-intervening-install 0-hop 4-stage delay control (`delayed_4stage`: `1.9435` at 720st) confirms that passive 4-step staleness slightly *degrades* 0-hop WM (`+0.0029` BPB vs. 2-stage `1.9407`), so the `-0.0188` to `-0.0197` BPB improvements over `delayed_4stage` arise specifically from the post-install 1-hop MLP and 1-hop Attention-Value highways (while the Step-0/Step-120 cosine diagnostics in Section 4.9 Finding 3 evaluate a fixed-state simultaneous $\sigma = 10^{-3}, G = BT$ diagnostic across the 5 linear/separable matrix families on a 120-step Global-BP-warmed checkpoint rather than the deployed $\sigma = 0.02$ delayed pipeline with intervening installations or $W_Q, W_K$); and (e) in Proposition 11 (`exp78` & `exp79`), we highlight that neither the elementary Rademacher variance identity nor polar retraction is itself a new mathematical principle, that finite-$K_{\text{NS}}$ Newton–Schulz retraction maintains approximate column orthogonality ($V_l^\top V_l \approx I_r$), that `exp79` Arms 3–4 (`oja_lp_scalar` / `oja_lp_scalar_mh`) vs. Arms 5–6 compare two complete bridge recipes (differing in Step-1 seeding, bridge LRs `0.006/0.006` vs. `0.04/0.004`, column-normalized encoder $U_{\text{dir}}$, and 1-call input-space scalar contraction vs. 2-call local bridge pullback), and that there is a complementary quality–time and pipeline-depth trade-off between **3-Stage `Oja-LP-WM`** (`t-2` pipeline depth, zero reverse error matmuls through Transformer backbone weights, zero local attention pullbacks $A_i^\top u_v$, using a 2-call local bridge pullback $V_l(U_l^\top e_l)$ on the block-local rank-$r$ auxiliary bridge $(U_l, V_l)$, `32.78s` on `d6` 720st reaching `1.9371 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB and `61.58s` / `68.11s` on 12-layer `d12` 720st reaching `1.9337 ± 0.0007` / `1.9335 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB—statistically significantly beating 6-layer `d6` 720st `Oja-LP-WM` by `-0.0036 ± 0.0009` BPB, 95% CI `[-0.0058, -0.0013]`, $p = 0.0209$, and running $2.08\times$–$2.30\times$ faster than 4-stage `d12` Dual 1-Hop WM at a `+0.0096` BPB quality gap), **4-Stage Class I `1-Hop MLP Highway WM`** (`t-4` pipeline depth, zero backbone reverse matmuls, zero $A_i^\top u_v$, and zero bridge pullbacks, `52.58s` / `54.85s` on `d6` 720st reaching `1.9355` / `1.9362` DEV BPB), and **4-Stage Class II `Superposed Dual 1-Hop Highway WM`** (`t-4` pipeline depth, local causal attention gather $\tilde{u}_v = A_i^\top u_v$, `47.02s` / `73.17s` on `d6` 720st reaching `1.9247` / `1.9238 ± 0.0008` DEV BPB and `141.59s` on 12-layer `d12` 720st reaching `1.9239 ± 0.0006` DEV BPB).

## 6. Reproducibility and Supplement Manifest

The attached `supplement.zip` ($1.91\text{ MB}$ uncompressed, $0.53\text{ MB}$ compressed; strictly under the $2,000,000$-byte limit) contains `figure-1.png`, all self-contained training scripts and helper modules (including `credit_decay_probe.py`, `exp77_1hop_mlp_wm_engine.py`, `exp77_surrogate_and_wm_core.py`, `exp77_dual_1hop_highway_wm.py`, `audit_exp77_mechanisms.py`, `exp78_local_predictive_wm.py`, and `exp79_oja_stiefel_bridge_and_d12_scaling.py`), retained 3-seed JSON records (where `exp77`–`exp79` retain 2-point `start, end` per-seed `dev_bpb` checkpoint trajectories, `first, last` step `bridge_r2_explained_var` entries in `exp79`, and arm-level `dev_bpb_mean, valb_bpb_mean, dev_ppl_mean`; `exp59`–`exp76` retain 3-point `start, midpoint, end` per-seed `dev_bpb` checkpoint trajectories and top-level audit summary means via `build_paper5_supplement.py` with full series permanently archived in `/papers/general_pretraining_20/versions/9`–`15`; and `exp26`–`exp58` retain full per-seed terminal metrics and final checkpoints with intermediate series archived in `/papers/general_pretraining_20/versions/8`–`12`), uncontended timing and isolated kernel-substitution benchmarks, geometric audit code, figure generation code, and a pure-Python verification script:
- `verify_paper5.py`: Runs in `< 0.5` seconds using only the Python 3 standard library (`python3 verify_paper5.py`). It verifies the **algebraic, expectation, and variance identities of Propositions 1–11 alongside the retained diagnostic and experimental JSON records** (`Checks 1–4H`, including exhaustive $2^N$ and $2^D$ enumerations, Proposition 1c's $D=3$ 8-pattern enumeration verifying $\mathbb{E}[P_x\hat{\mu}_{\text{WM}}^{(1)}] = (1/4, -1/4, 1)^\top \neq \mu_{\text{skip}} = (1/2, -1/2, 1)^\top$, closed-form Stage-2 leverage inversion $\operatorname{diag}(1-x_i^2/D)^{-1}$ restoring exact equality $\cos = 1.000000$, and outer-product Frobenius discrepancy bound $\left(\frac{\max_i x_i^2}{D}\right)\frac{\|a_n\|_2 \|h_n\|_2}{\text{rms}(z_n)}$, causal attention commutation, diagonal skip identity, sparse vs. bounded-kurtosis `RMSNorm` expectation bounds, Proposition 5's unprojected vs. tangent-projected reward-reconstruction variance identity, Proposition 6's closed-form Wiener-MMSE key-gather optimality, 3-token Rademacher enumeration, Walsh-Hadamard $H_4$ probe multiplexing orthogonality, and Sparse Top-$K=32$ energy retention, **Proposition 7's restatement/application of Zhang et al.
